While the data section is scrambled because it's binary, I can extract some of the actual values present in your file for your reference:

*   **Product Names:** `PUB IND UNI 181D-10YRS`, `TDTDR PUB IND UNI-91-179D-INR`
*   **Branch Names:** `VIKRAM SARABHAI BHAWAN`, `MANDVI MUMBAI`, `KHOPAT BRANCH THANE`, `WADALA (MUMBAI)`, `DHARAVI (MUMBAI)`, `ACB BARAGAON`, `CHAKALA ROAD BRANCH`, `NALLASOPARA - WEST`, `PANVEL`.
*   **Circle Name:** `MUMBAI METRO`, `LUCKNOW`.
*   **Dates found:** `1961-09-04`, `2011-08-30`, `2026-08-30`, `2027-08-31`, etc.

**Note:** If you need to extract the actual data into a spreadsheet or a clean table, you cannot do it by copying and pasting the text. You must open the original `.db` or `.sqlite` file using a tool like **DB Browser for SQLite** or by using Python's `sqlite3` library.


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\docs\add-database.md
####################################################################################################





This page walks you through connecting a second (or third) SQLite database to the pipeline with **minimal code changes** — the architecture is designed so that adding a database is almost entirely a configuration task.

**Total files to edit: 2** (plus your `.db` file itself).

---

## Overview

The pipeline discovers databases at runtime by:

1. `SchemaChecker` scanning the `data_root` folder for `*.db` files and scoring each one against the question.
2. `SQLWriterAgent` reading table metadata (descriptions, column definitions, few-shots) from `genai/config/databases.toml`.

You do **not** need to touch any Python files.

---


Put the SQLite database file anywhere under `dlh_ai/data/`:

```
dlh_ai/data/
├── comp_db/
│   └── COMP_REPORTS.db          ← existing
└── my_new_db/
    └── MY_NEW_DATABASE.db       ← new file
```

> The exact sub-folder name does not matter — `SchemaChecker` uses `data_root.glob("**/*.db")`.

If you need to create the database from a CSV or schema script, run that first and place the `.db` file here.

---


Open `dlh_ai/genai/config/databases.toml` and add a new `[databases.<key>]` block:

```toml
[databases.COMP_REPORTS]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db"
description = "Banking compliance reports database for term-deposit products."

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL]
description  = "..."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE ACCT_BRNCH IN ({branch_code})"

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL.columns]
BRCD = "BRANCH CODE OR BRANCH NUMBER"


[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE]
db_file     = "data/my_new_db/MY_NEW_DATABASE.db"
description = "Short description of what this database contains."

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE]
description  = "What this table represents."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE BRANCH_ID = '{branch_id}'"

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE.columns]
BRANCH_ID    = "Unique identifier for the branch"
CUSTOMER_ID  = "Unique customer identifier"
PRODUCT_TYPE = "Type of financial product"
BALANCE      = "Current account balance in INR"
STATUS       = "Account status: ACTIVE, CLOSED, DORMANT"

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: Show all active accounts in MY_TABLE.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE';
<end_sql_response>

Question: Count accounts by product type.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT PRODUCT_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM MY_TABLE GROUP BY PRODUCT_TYPE ORDER BY cnt DESC;
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

### Field reference

| Field | Required | Description |
|-------|----------|-------------|
| `db_file` | ✓ | Relative path to the `.db` file from the **project root** (`dlh_ai/`) |
| `description` | recommended | Injected into the LLM prompt as context |
| `tables.<NAME>.description` | recommended | Per-table description in the LLM prompt |
| `tables.<NAME>.filter_query` | optional | Template for branch-filtered queries |
| `tables.<NAME>.columns.<COL>` | recommended | Business description of each column |
| `tables.<NAME>.few_shots.examples` | strongly recommended | NL→SQL examples for this table |

---


By default the app uses the database configured under `DATABASE__DB_FOLDER` / `DATABASE__DB_NAME` in `.env` (or `AppSettings` defaults). If you want **this** new database to be the default:

```dotenv
# .env
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/my_new_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=MY_NEW_DATABASE.db
```

If you want to keep `COMP_REPORTS.db` as the default but also support the new one, **no `.env` change is needed** — `SchemaChecker` automatically routes questions to the best-matching database based on keyword overlap.

---

## Step 4 — Verify

```python
python -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'dlh_ai')
from utils.registry import _build_db_registry
reg = _build_db_registry()
print(list(reg.keys()))
"
```

You should see `MY_TABLE` (or whatever your table name is) alongside `COMP_TBL`.

---

## Step 5 — Test

Restart Streamlit, then ask a question specific to the new table and check the **Agent Trace** in the result — you should see `schema_checker:db=MY_NEW_DATABASE.db`.

---

## Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |
|---------|-------------|-----|
| SchemaChecker still uses old DB | Keyword scores tied | Add more specific question keywords in `few_shots.examples` |
| `MY_TABLE` not in registry | Typo in `databases.toml` table key | Key must exactly match the SQLite table name |
| `FileNotFoundError` in registry | Wrong `db_file` path | Path is relative to `dlh_ai/` project root, not to `genai/config/` |
| SQL always wrong for new table | No few-shots | Add at least 3 diverse `few_shots.examples` |
| Column names rejected by reviewer | Table not in `sqlite_master` | Ensure the `.db` file exists and the table was created |

---

## Summary

| # | File | What to add |
|---|------|-------------|
| 1 | `dlh_ai/data/<folder>/<NAME>.db` | The SQLite database file itself |
| 2 | `dlh_ai/genai/config/databases.toml` | One `[databases.<KEY>]` block per database, one `[tables.<NAME>]` block per table |

That's it. No Python changes required.

---


If your new table should be the **only** table the pipeline ever queries:

1. Set `DATABASE__DB_NAME` in `.env` to your new `.db` file.
2. Set `COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME` in `dlh_ai/utils/config.py`:

```python
COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME: str = "MY_TABLE"   # was "COMP_TBL"
```

3. Update `SQLWriterAgent` default in `dlh_ai/genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py`:

```python
class SQLWriterAgent:
    def __init__(
        self,
        model_service: ModelLoaderService,
        table_name: str = "MY_TABLE",    # was COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME
    ) -> None:
```


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\docs\agents.md
####################################################################################################




# Agent Nodes

The NL-to-SQL pipeline is a six-node **LangGraph StateGraph**.  
Every node receives the full `AgentState` dict, reads the keys it needs, and returns a **partial** dict containing only the keys it owns.

---


`genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py`

```python
class AgentState(TypedDict, total=False):
    # ── Input ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    question:             str          # raw user question
    question_id:          str | None   # optional idempotency key

    # ── Node 1 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    interpreted_question: str          # normalised question with trailing ?
    intent_ok:            bool         # True = proceed, False = clarify
    clarification_needed: str | None   # message shown to the user when False

    # ── Node 2 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    db_path:              str          # absolute path to the chosen .db file
    schema_context:       str          # Markdown schema block injected into LLM prompt

    # ── Node 3 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    sql_query:            str          # raw SQL returned by the LLM

    # ── Node 4 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    sql_review_passed:    bool
    review_feedback:      str | None   # sent back to Node 3 on retry
    retry_count:          int

    # ── Node 4.5 outputs ───────────────────────────────────
    sqlglot_valid:        bool
    sqlglot_error:        str | None

    # ── Node 5 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    result_rows:          list[dict[str, object]]
    result_columns:       list[str]
    result_summary:       str

    # ── Shared bookkeeping ─────────────────────────────────
    error:                str | None
    stages:               list[str]    # append-only audit trail (LangGraph reducer)
```

The `stages` field uses `Annotated[list[str], operator.add]` so LangGraph **appends** each node's entries rather than overwriting the whole list.

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/intent_checker.yaml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

Runs four sequential checks. The first check that fails returns `intent_ok=False` immediately; all four must pass for the pipeline to continue.

| Check | Rule | Failure example |
|-------|------|----------------|
| **Empty** | question is blank after strip | `""` or `"   "` |
| **Disallowed action** | token matches DROP / DELETE / INSERT / UPDATE / TRUNCATE / ALTER / CREATE / REPLACE / PRAGMA | `"delete all accounts"` |
| **Too short** | fewer than 3 meaningful tokens **and** no metric term | `"pending"` |
| **Vague / ambiguous** | phrase is in `_VAGUE_PHRASES` set, or contains an ambiguous pronoun with no metric term | `"give me data"`, `"please verify this"` |

If the question contains any of the 24 metric terms (`age`, `kyc`, `balance`, `maturity`, `account`, `count`, `sum`, `top` …) the too-short check is skipped, allowing short but specific questions like `"show KYC"`.

### Outputs written

```
interpreted_question   — stripped + trailing "?" added
intent_ok              — True / False
clarification_needed   — None or YAML message string
stages                 — ["intent_checker:ok"] or ["intent_checker:too_short"] etc.
```

### Routing

```
intent_ok=True  → Node 2 (SchemaChecker)
intent_ok=False → clarify_node → END
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/schema_checker.yaml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

1. Scans `data_root` (the DB folder) for every `*.db` file.
2. Scores each file by keyword overlap between the question and table/column names.
3. Selects the highest-scoring `.db` file.
4. Builds a **Markdown schema context** string containing:
   - Table names with column types (from `PRAGMA table_info`)
   - Sample rows (up to `schema.sample_row_limit` from `schema_checker.yaml`, default 3)
   - Column business descriptions from `databases.toml`


```
score = 1  if db_filename_stem is in the question (case-insensitive)
     += 2  for each table name found in the question
     += 1  for each column name found in the question
```

### Outputs written

```
db_path        — absolute path to the winning .db file
schema_context — Markdown string (injected verbatim into the LLM prompt)
stages         — ["schema_checker:db=COMP_REPORTS.db"]
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/sql_writer.yaml`  
**Type:** **LLM call** (only node that calls the LLM)

### What it does

1. `ContextBuilder.build()` assembles the full prompt from:
   - System role (from `sql_writer.yaml`)
   - Numbered rules list
   - Table description + column block (from `databases.toml`)
   - Live schema context (from Node 2)
   - Few-shot examples (from `databases.toml`)
   - Reviewer feedback (present only on retries)
2. Calls `ModelLoaderService.generate(prompt)`.
3. Extracts SQL from between `<begin_sql_response>` … `<end_sql_response>` tags.

### Retry behaviour

On retry (coming back from Node 4 or 4.5), `review_feedback` is non-null and gets injected as a `[REVIEWER FEEDBACK]` block at the bottom of the prompt, so the LLM can see exactly what was wrong.

### Outputs written

```
sql_query    — extracted SQL string (or "" if extraction failed)
retry_count  — unchanged (passed through)
stages       — ["sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0"]
error        — set only if no SQL tags found in output
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/sql_review.yaml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

Six checks in order:

| # | Check | Action on fail |
|---|-------|---------------|
| 1 | `retry_count >= max_retries` (default 3) | Sets `error`, routes to Executor to surface the message |
| 2 | SQL is empty | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 3 | Contains disallowed DML/DDL keyword | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 4 | Does not start with `SELECT` | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 5 | Missing `FROM` clause | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 6 | Referenced tables don't exist in the `.db` file | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |

### Routing

```
error set        → Executor (surfaces the error message to user)
passed=True      → Node 4.5 (SQLValidator)
passed=False     → Node 3 (SQLWriter, retry)
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py`  
**Type:** Rule-based — uses **sqlglot** AST parser (no LLM call)

### What it does

Three checks:

1. **Parse validity** — `sqlglot.parse()` must succeed without raising `ParseError`.
2. **Statement type** — parsed AST root must be a `Select` node.
3. **Dialect canonicalisation** — rewrites the SQL to canonical SQLite syntax via `stmt.sql(dialect="sqlite")`.

If sqlglot is not installed the node passes transparently (`sqlglot_valid=True`).

### Routing

```
valid   → Executor
invalid → Node 3 (SQLWriter, retry with sqlglot error in feedback)
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py`  
**Config:** `genai/config/query.toml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

1. Applies the row-limit policy from `query.toml`:
   - If `enforce_default_limit=true` and the SQL has no `LIMIT` clause, appends `LIMIT <default_limit>`.
2. Opens a `BEGIN DEFERRED` transaction.
3. Runs `cursor.fetchmany(max_display_rows)` — hard ceiling regardless of SQL.
4. **Always rolls back** (unconditional in `finally`).
5. Builds the result summary string.


```python
conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")   # deferred = no write locks acquired
conn.rollback()                  # always, even on success
```

Even if a malicious SQL somehow bypassed all prior guards, the rollback ensures no data is ever written.

### Outputs written

```
result_rows     — list of {column: value} dicts
result_columns  — ordered list of column names
result_summary  — one-sentence human-readable summary
error           — None on success, error message on failure
stages          — ["executor_agent:ok rows=42"]
```

---

## Adding a New Node

1. Create `genai/nl2sql_agent/my_node.py` with a class that has `.run(state: AgentState) -> AgentState`.
2. Add a `my_node.yaml` under `genai/prompt/` (optional, only if it has user-facing messages).
3. In `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py`:
   - Import your node class.
   - Add `graph.add_node("my_node", MyNode().run)`.
   - Wire it with `graph.add_edge(...)` or `graph.add_conditional_edges(...)`.

# Architecture

This document covers the system from five views.

| # | Section | What it shows |
|---|---------|---------------|
| 0 | [Detailed System Flow Diagram](#0-detailed-system-flow--architecture) | Visual end-to-end architecture: UI → Prompt Engine → LLM → SQL Engine |
| 1 | [Folder Tree](#1-folder-tree) | Every file/folder with one-line annotations |
| 2 | [Component Layers](#2-component-layers) | Logical tiers: UI → Orchestration → Pipeline → Storage |
| 3 | [LangGraph Pipeline Flow](#3-langgraph-pipeline-flow) | Node-by-node state-graph with all routing edges |
| 4 | [AgentState Data-Flow](#4-agentstate-data-flow) | Which fields each node reads and writes |
| 5 | [Config Resolution Order](#5-config-resolution-order) | How `.env` overrides TOML, and TOML overrides code defaults |

---


> End-to-end view: **Interface & Session** → **Validation & Prompt Engineering** → **Model & Execution** → response back to the user.

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗

 │  application/app.py          │   │  Validation & Context Layer  │   │  Model & Execution Layer             │
 │  Interface & Session Layer   │   │                              │   │                                      │

 │  • Chat page + stage pills   │   │    Response                  │   │  • Configurable model name           │
 │  • Displays tabular / JSON   │   │  • Pydantic field validation │   │  • SSL / API key / timeout           │

 │    processor, messages,      │   │    compliance_bot.log        │   │  • COMP_REPORTS.db  ·  COMP_TBL      │
 │    last_csv                  │   │  • DEBUG / ERROR tracing     │   │  • SQLReviewer: regex + table checks │
 │                              │   │                              │   │  • SQLValidator: sqlglot AST parse   │
 │                              │   │  ── ContextBuilder ──        │   │  • Executes SELECT in               │
 │                              │   │  • Table descriptions from   │   │    BEGIN DEFERRED txn                │
 │                              │   │    databases.toml            │   │  • fetchmany(500) hard row cap       │
 │                              │   │  • Assembles column metadata │   │  • Unconditional ROLLBACK            │
 │                              │   │  • Compliance business rules │   │  • Formats JSON / result_summary     │
 │                              │   │  • Few-shot SQL examples     │   │                                      │
 │                              │   │  • Live PRAGMA schema ctx    │   │                                      │
 │                              │   │  • Global config:            │   │                                      │
 │                              │   │    genai/config/*.toml       │   │                                      │
 └──────────────────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────────────────┘
           │                                      │                                    │
           │                    Validate & Pass ──┼──── Build Prompt ─────────────────►│
           │                                      │                                    │
           │◄─────────────────── Structured Response ───────────────────────────────── │
           │    { sql_query · df_output · result_summary · stages · error }            │
           │                                                                            │

 ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

 ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

                              ┌─────────┐
                              │  START  │
                              └────┬────┘
                                   │  AgentState { question }
                                   ▼
                      ┌────────────────────────────┐
                      │  ① IntentChecker            │  rule-based · no LLM
                      │  intent_checker.py          │  4 keyword checks
                      └───────────┬────────────────┘
                                  │
               ┌──────────────────┴─────────────────┐
               │ intent_ok = True                   │ intent_ok = False
               ▼                                    ▼

  │  sql_reviewer.py           │                                   │
  └───────────┬────────────────┘                                   │
              │                                                     │
    ┌─────────┼──────────────────────┐                             │
    │         │ passed = True        │ passed = False              │
    │         │                      │ retry_count += 1 ───────────┘
    │         ▼                      │
    │  ┌────────────────────────┐    │
    │  │  ④½ SQLValidator       │    │◄──────────────────────────────┐

    │  │  sqlglot AST parse     │    │                               │

    │              │                 │                               │
    │     ┌────────┴──────┐          │                               │
    │     │ valid         │ invalid  │                               │
    │     │               │ retry_count += 1 ──────────────────────-┘
    │     ▼               │
    │  ┌────────────────────────┐
    └─►│  ⑤ ExecutorAgent       │  BEGIN DEFERRED txn
       │  executor_agent.py     │  fetchmany(500) row cap
       │  read-only SQLite      │  unconditional ROLLBACK
       └───────────┬────────────┘
                   │  result_rows · result_columns · result_summary · stages
                   ▼
              ┌─────────┐
              │   END   │
              └─────────┘
```

**End-to-end data flow:**

```
 User types question
 (Streamlit st.chat_input)
        │
        ▼  LAYER 1 — Interface & Session
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │  application/app.py                                                  │
 │  st.session_state.processor                                          │
 │    .process_query_with_orchestration(ComplianceChatRequest)          │
 └─────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │  AgentState { question, question_id }
                               ▼  LAYER 2 — Validation & Prompt Engineering
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │  LangGraph StateGraph  (genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py)                 │
 │                                                                      │
 │  ① intent_checker      rule-based, no LLM  →  intent_ok flag        │
 │         │                                                            │
 │  ② schema_checker      PRAGMA queries      →  db_path, schema_ctx   │
 │         │                                                            │
 │  ③ sql_writer_agent ★  DLH LLM call        →  sql_query             │
 │         │              (only LLM node)                               │
 │  ④ sql_reviewer        regex + DB checks   →  review_feedback       │
 │         │              (retry → ③ if fail)                           │
 │  ④½ sql_validator      sqlglot AST parse   →  canonical SQL         │
 │         │              (retry → ③ if fail)                           │
 │  ⑤ executor_agent      SQLite SELECT       →  result_rows           │
 └─────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼  LAYER 3 — Model & Execution
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │  ModelLoaderService  (genai/core/model.py)                           │
 │  DLH LLM  ──generates SQL──►  SQLite COMP_REPORTS.db (COMP_TBL)     │
 └─────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼  Back to LAYER 1
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │  ComplianceOrchestrationResponse                                     │
 │  { sql_query, df_output, result_summary, stages, error }             │
 │                                                                      │
 │  Streamlit renders:                                                  │
 │    • Stage pills: Intent → Schema → SQL Writer → Review → Validator  │
 │                   → Executor                                         │
 │    • Generated SQL block                                             │
 │    • Result data table (JSON → DataFrame)                            │
 │    • ↓  Download as CSV button                                       │
 └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 1. Folder Tree

```
COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH-V3-STREAMLIT/       ← workspace root (run all commands from here)
│
├── dlh_ai/
│   ├── application/
│   │   ├── app.py                  ← Streamlit app — single entry-point (UI + pipeline)
│   │   └── fonts/                  ← Urbanist TTF files embedded as base64 in app.py
│   │
│   ├── data/
│   │   ├── comp_db/
│   │   │   └── COMP_REPORTS.db     ← Primary SQLite DB (seed script creates this)
│   │   ├── logs/
│   │   │   └── compliance_bot.log  ← Rotating log — always written here (10 MB × 10)
│   │   └── seed_synthetic_data.py  ← CLI — creates + seeds COMP_REPORTS.db
│   │
│   ├── genai/
│   │   ├── config/                 ← Static TOML — no secrets ever here
│   │   │   ├── application.toml    ← App display name
│   │   │   ├── database.toml       ← DB type, folder, filename
│   │   │   ├── databases.toml  ★★★ ← Table registry: columns + few-shots per table
│   │   │   ├── llm.toml            ← Provider, model name, temperature defaults
│   │   │   ├── logging.toml        ← Python logging dictConfig (handlers, levels)
│   │   │   ├── query.toml          ← Row-limit policy (enforce_default_limit)
│   │   │   └── ui.toml             ← Legacy UI config (informational only)
│   │   │
│   │   ├── core/
│   │   │   ├── exceptions.py       ← Exception hierarchy (ComplianceBotError base)
│   │   │   ├── model.py            ← ModelLoaderService + ProviderManager
│   │   │   ├── processor.py        ← ComplianceProcessor — graph orchestration
│   │   │   └── schemas.py          ← Pydantic response models + factory helpers
│   │   │
│   │   ├── nl2sql_agent/
│   │   │   ├── state.py            ← AgentState TypedDict — shared pipeline state
│   │   │   ├── graph.py        ★★★ ← Builds & compiles the LangGraph StateGraph
│   │   │   ├── intent_checker.py   ← Node 1 — rule-based intent classification
│   │   │   ├── schema_checker.py   ← Node 2 — DB selection + Markdown schema
│   │   │   ├── sql_writer_agent.py ← Node 3 — LLM call + SQL extraction
│   │   │   ├── sql_reviewer.py     ← Node 4 — regex + table-existence checks
│   │   │   ├── sql_validator.py    ← Node 4.5 — sqlglot AST parse + dialect
│   │   │   └── executor_agent.py   ← Node 5 — read-only SQLite + result formatting
│   │   │
│   │   └── prompt/                 ← YAML prompt files — edit to change LLM wording
│   │       ├── intent_checker.yaml ← User-facing messages for Node 1
│   │       ├── schema_checker.yaml ← Headers + error messages for Node 2
│   │       ├── sql_writer.yaml     ← System role, rules, prompt template for Node 3
│   │       ├── sql_review.yaml     ← Feedback strings for Node 4
│   │       ├── common.yaml         ← Shared boilerplate
│   │       ├── compliance.yaml     ← Compliance-domain context strings
│   │       ├── report_generation.yaml
│   │       └── system.yaml         ← Top-level system persona
│   │
│   ├── utils/
│   │   ├── config.py               ← Pydantic BaseSettings (AppSettings, ModelSettings…)
│   │   ├── helpers.py              ← Stateless stdlib helpers (JSON, I/O, truncate…)
│   │   ├── logger.py               ← get_logger() factory — reads logging.toml
│   │   ├── registry.py             ← DB registry + SQLite helpers + YAML loader
│   │   └── __init__.py             ← Re-exports everything above
│   │
│   ├── docs/                       ← documentation (this folder)
│   └── requirements.txt
│
└── llm_config.json                 ← Persisted credentials (auto-created by Streamlit app)
```

---


```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║  PRESENTATION LAYER                                              ║
║                                                                  ║
║   application/app.py  (Streamlit)                                ║
║   • Page: LLM Configuration — form to test & save DLH creds     ║
║   • Page: Chat — st.chat_input, stage pills, CSV download        ║
║   • Persists LLM credentials to llm_config.json                  ║
║   • st.session_state stores processor, messages, last_csv        ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  ORCHESTRATION LAYER                                             ║
║                                                                  ║
║   ComplianceProcessor  (genai/core/processor.py)                 ║
║   • Builds AgentState from user question                         ║
║   • Calls compiled LangGraph graph via .invoke()                 ║
║   • Maps final AgentState → ComplianceOrchestrationResponse      ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  PIPELINE LAYER  (LangGraph StateGraph)                          ║
║                                                                  ║
║   Node 1   IntentChecker    rule-based, no LLM                   ║
║   Node 2   SchemaChecker    rule-based, no LLM                   ║
║   Node 3   SQLWriterAgent   ★ the only LLM call                  ║
║   Node 4   SQLReviewer      rule-based, no LLM                   ║
║   Node 4.5 SQLValidator     sqlglot AST, no LLM                  ║
║   Node 5   ExecutorAgent    rule-based, no LLM                   ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  INFRASTRUCTURE LAYER                                            ║
║                                                                  ║
║   utils/config.py    Pydantic Settings — reads .env              ║
║   utils/logger.py    get_logger() — reads logging.toml           ║
║   utils/registry.py  YAML loader + SQLite helpers + DB registry  ║
║   utils/helpers.py   stateless stdlib helpers                    ║
║                                                                  ║
║   genai/config/*.toml   TOML config (no secrets)                 ║
║   genai/prompt/*.yaml   YAML prompt files (LLM wording)          ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  STORAGE LAYER                                                   ║
║                                                                  ║
║   data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db   SQLite — read-only at runtime   ║
║   data/logs/compliance_bot.log   Rotating log file               ║
║   llm_config.json                Credentials from UI             ║
║   .env                           Secrets (never committed)        ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
```

---


Full node graph with every conditional edge.

```
                        ┌─────────┐
                        │  START  │
                        └────┬────┘
                             │  AgentState{question}
                             ▼
                  ┌──────────────────────┐
                  │   1. IntentChecker   │  rule-based (4 checks)
                  │   intent_checker.py  │  no LLM call
                  └──────────┬───────────┘
                             │
              ┌──────────────┴──────────────┐
              │ intent_ok=True              │ intent_ok=False
              ▼                             ▼
 ┌────────────────────────┐      ┌──────────────────────┐
 │   2. SchemaChecker     │      │    clarify_node       │
 │   schema_checker.py    │      │  (terminal — returns  │
 │   Scans *.db files,    │      │   clarification msg)  │
 │   scores by keyword    │      └──────────┬────────────┘
 │   overlap, builds      │                 │
 │   Markdown schema      │                 ▼
 └──────────┬─────────────┘             ┌───────┐
            │                           │  END  │
            ▼                           └───────┘
 ┌────────────────────────┐  ◄──────────────────────────┐
 │   3. SQLWriterAgent    │                             │
 │   sql_writer_agent.py  │  retry (with feedback       │
 │   ★ ONLY LLM CALL ★    │  injected into prompt)      │
 └──────────┬─────────────┘                             │
            │ sql_query                                 │
            ▼                                           │
 ┌────────────────────────┐                             │
 │   4. SQLReviewer       │  rule-based (6 checks)      │
 │   sql_reviewer.py      │  no LLM call                │
 └──────────┬─────────────┘                             │
            │                                           │
  ┌─────────┼──────────────────┐                        │
  │         │                  │                        │
  │ error   │ passed=True      │ passed=False            │
  │ (max    │                  │ retry_count += 1       │
  │ retries)│                  └────────────────────────┘
  │         ▼                                           ▲
  │  ┌────────────────────────┐                         │
  │  │  4.5 SQLValidator      │  uses sqlglot AST       │
  │  │  sql_validator.py      │  no LLM call            │
  │  └──────────┬─────────────┘                         │
  │             │                                       │
  │     ┌───────┴───────┐                               │
  │     │ valid         │ invalid                       │
  │     │               │ retry_count += 1              │
  │     │               └───────────────────────────────┘
  │     ▼
  │  ┌────────────────────────┐
  └─►│   5. ExecutorAgent     │  BEGIN DEFERRED
     │   executor_agent.py    │  fetchmany(max_display_rows)
     │   Read-only SQLite,    │  unconditional ROLLBACK
     └──────────┬─────────────┘
                │ result_rows, result_summary, stages
                ▼
            ┌───────┐
            │  END  │
            └───────┘
```

**Typical latency breakdown:**

| Step | Time |
|------|------|
| IntentChecker (rule-based) | < 1 ms |
| SchemaChecker (PRAGMA queries) | 5–20 ms |
| SQLWriterAgent (LLM call) | 1–5 s |
| SQLReviewer (rule-based) | < 1 ms |
| SQLValidator (sqlglot) | < 5 ms |
| ExecutorAgent (SQLite SELECT) | 5–50 ms |
| **Total** | **~2–6 s** |

---


Which fields each node **reads** (→ in) and **writes** (← out).

```
                        AgentState keys
                        ───────────────
question ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────►
question_id ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────►

              Node 1: IntentChecker
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  question                                   │
              │ writes: interpreted_question                       │
              │         intent_ok                                  │
              │         clarification_needed                       │
              │         stages += ["intent_checker:ok"]            │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 2: SchemaChecker
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  interpreted_question (or question)          │
              │ writes: db_path                                     │
              │         schema_context                              │
              │         stages += ["schema_checker:db=…"]          │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 3: SQLWriterAgent
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  interpreted_question                        │
              │         schema_context                              │
              │         review_feedback   (None on first pass)      │
              │         retry_count                                 │
              │ writes: sql_query                                   │
              │         stages += ["sql_writer_agent:llm_generated"]│
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 4: SQLReviewer
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  sql_query                                   │
              │         db_path                                     │
              │         retry_count                                 │
              │ writes: sql_review_passed                           │
              │         review_feedback                             │
              │         retry_count  (incremented on fail)          │
              │         error        (only if max retries hit)      │
              │         stages += ["sql_reviewer:passed"]           │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 4.5: SQLValidator
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  sql_query                                   │
              │         retry_count                                 │
              │ writes: sqlglot_valid                               │
              │         sqlglot_error                               │
              │         sql_query   (canonicalised SQLite dialect)  │
              │         retry_count (incremented on fail)           │
              │         stages += ["sql_validator:passed"]          │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 5: ExecutorAgent
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  sql_query                                   │
              │         db_path                                     │
              │ writes: result_rows                                 │
              │         result_columns                              │
              │         result_summary                              │
              │         error   (None on success)                   │
              │         stages += ["executor_agent:ok rows=N"]      │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

result_rows ◄────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
result_columns ◄─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
result_summary ◄─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
stages ◄─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
error ◄──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
```

---


Settings are layered — later sources win.

```
Priority   Source                     Mechanism
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  LOW   genai/config/*.toml       Default values in TOML files.
        ─────────────────────     Loaded by utils/registry.py and
                                  utils/logger.py at import time.

          ▼ overridden by

        utils/config.py           Python-level defaults in Pydantic
        (Field default=…)         Field() declarations.

          ▼ overridden by

        .env                      Local/deployment secrets.
        ─────────────────────     Loaded by pydantic-settings.
                                  Never committed to git.

          ▼ overridden by

  HIGH  Environment variables     Injected by CI, Docker, CML.
        (process environment)     e.g. MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=abc123

          ▼ overridden at runtime by

        llm_config.json           Credentials saved by the Streamlit
                                  LLM Configuration page.
                                  Loaded by app.py on startup.
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Nested key syntax (pydantic-settings env_nested_delimiter="__"):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  Python class           .env key                            │
  │  ─────────────          ─────────────────────────────────── │
  │  AppSettings.port       PORT                                │
  │  AppSettings.log_level  LOG_LEVEL                           │
  │  database.db_folder     DATABASE__DB_FOLDER                 │
  │  database.db_name       DATABASE__DB_NAME                   │
  │  model.model_name       MODEL__MODEL_NAME                   │
  │  model.dlh_base_url     MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL                 │
  │  model.dlh_api_key      MODEL__DLH_API_KEY                  │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## Design Decisions

| Decision | Rationale |
|----------|-----------|
| Streamlit replaces FastAPI + run_ui.py | Single process, single port, no proxy layer — simpler to deploy |
| `llm_config.json` for credential persistence | Survives Streamlit hot-reloads and server restarts without `.env` changes |
| All config in TOML + `.env`, zero hard-coded strings | Environment-specific values never touch source code |
| YAML prompts loaded via `utils/registry.py` | Change LLM wording without touching Python; hot-reload via `reload()` |
| `total=False` TypedDict for AgentState | Each node writes only its own keys; LangGraph merges correctly |
| sqlglot AST validation **after** regex review | Defence in depth — catches syntax errors the regex misses |
| `BEGIN DEFERRED` + unconditional rollback in executor | SQLite stays read-only regardless of what SQL reaches the executor |
| Logs always anchored to `<project_root>/data/logs/` | Consistent location regardless of which directory the server starts from |


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\docs\codebase-reference.md
####################################################################################################

# Codebase Reference

Every file and folder in the project with a concise description of what it is and what it does.

---

## Top-level

| Path | What it does |
|---|---|
| `dlh_ai/` | Python package root — the entire application lives here. |
| `llm_config.json` | LLM credentials saved by the Streamlit UI at runtime (`model_name`, `base_url`, `api_key`). Auto-created on first *Test & Save*; loaded by `app.py` on startup to pre-fill the form. |
| `.env` | Local secrets and environment overrides. Read by `pydantic-settings` at import time. Never committed to git. |
| `dlh_ai/requirements.txt` | Pinned Python dependencies for the entire project. |
| `dlh_ai/pyproject.toml` | Project metadata (`name`, `version`, `requires-python`) and tool configuration for `ruff` and `mypy`. |
| `dlh_ai/llm_config.json` | *(same as root-level)*: persisted LLM credentials written by the Streamlit UI. |
| `dlh_ai/README.md` | Short project overview and quickstart instructions. |

---

## `application/`

The Streamlit user interface. This folder is the only thing a browser-facing user ever interacts with.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `app.py` | Single-file Streamlit application. Renders two pages: **LLM Configuration** (form to enter and test DLH model credentials) and **Chat** (question input, pipeline stage pills, SQL block, results table, CSV download). Holds `processor`, `messages`, and `last_csv` in `st.session_state`. Calls `ComplianceProcessor.process_query_with_orchestration()` on every user message. |
| `launcher.py` | Thin subprocess wrapper. Resolves `app.py`'s path relative to `__file__` and launches `streamlit run` bound to `CDSW_APP_PORT` (default `8551`). Used when the app is deployed as a CML Application. |

---

## `data/`

Storage and data-access layer. Contains the Iceberg target table helpers, backend engines, and the synthetic data pipeline.

### `data/` — engine files

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `__init__.py` | Package init. Re-exports `PandasEngine`, `PolarsEngine`, `SparkEngine`, `HDFSClient`, and `CAIClient`. |
| `_pandas_backend.py` | `PandasEngine` dataclass. Wraps a CML Data (`cml.data_v1`) Hive/Impala connection. `read_table(sql)` executes a SELECT and returns a pandas DataFrame. `save_table(df, name)` batch-inserts a DataFrame into an Iceberg table via `INSERT … VALUES`. Also provides `read_csv_from_hdfs`, `read_parquet_from_hdfs`, `save_csv_to_hdfs`, `save_parquet`. |
| `_polars_backend.py` | `PolarsEngine` dataclass. Same connection pattern as `PandasEngine` but returns and accepts Polars DataFrames. Batch-inserts via `cursor.executemany`. |
| `_spark_backend.py` | `SparkEngine` dataclass. Creates a PySpark session. `read_table(name)` reads from the Spark catalog. `save_table(df, name, path)` writes as an Iceberg table. Also supports Spark MERGE via `merge_table(df, merge_query)`. |
| `seed_synthetic_data.py` | CLI script. Creates (or resets) a local SQLite database `comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` and bulk-inserts synthetic compliance records. Run with `--reset` to truncate first, `--count N` for row count. Uses only the Python standard library. |
| `datapush_read.ipynb` | Jupyter notebook. Generates `ROW_COUNT` (default 2 000) synthetic rows matching the 22-column Iceberg schema from `iceberg_schema.txt`, casts dtypes to Hive-compatible types, then pushes them to `dev_feature_store.COMP_TBL` via `PandasEngine.save_table()`. Final cells verify the push with a `SELECT … LIMIT 5` and a `COUNT(*)`. |

### `data/comp_db/`

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `COMP_REPORTS.db` | Local SQLite database created by `seed_synthetic_data.py`. Contains `COMP_TBL` with synthetic term-deposit compliance records. Used for local development and testing when the Iceberg cluster is unavailable. |
| `db_details.txt` | Human-readable notes about the SQLite schema and sample data values. Reference only — not read by any code. |

### `data/iceberg_schema.txt`

Iceberg DDL for `dev_feature_store.COMP_TBL`. Defines all 22 columns with Hive types (`STRING`, `DATE`, `INT`, `DECIMAL`). Read at import time by `schema_checker.py` to annotate the LLM schema context with exact Hive column types.

---

## `genai/`

All AI/ML logic. Three sub-packages plus the top-level pipeline entry-point.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `__init__.py` | Package init. Imports and re-exports `CompliancePipeline` and `QueryResult` at the top level. Sets `__version__ = "4.0.0"`. |
| `pipeline.py` | **User-facing entry-point.** `CompliancePipeline(model_name, base_url, api_key)` wraps the entire workflow. `.ask(question)` runs the full pipeline and always returns a `QueryResult`. `QueryResult` is a frozen dataclass with `ok`, `summary`, `sql`, `table` (pandas DataFrame), `columns`, `needs_clarification`, `clarification_hint`, `error_message` (what/expected/fix), `question_id`, and `stages`. Nothing internal leaks out. |

### `genai/core/`

Orchestration, LLM client, Pydantic models, and exceptions.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `__init__.py` | Re-exports all public symbols from the four modules below. Entry-point note: new code should use `CompliancePipeline` rather than `ComplianceProcessor` directly. |
| `exceptions.py` | Domain exception hierarchy. `ComplianceBotError` is the base; all subclasses carry `what`, `expected`, and `fix` string fields plus `.user_message()` which composes them into a structured error string. Subclasses: `ModelLoadError`, `ModelInferenceError`, `LLMProviderError`, `LLMTimeoutError`, `UnknownProviderError`, `SqlGenerationError`, `SqlExecutionError`, `DatabaseConnectionError`, `RecordNotFoundError`, `DataIngestionError`, `SchemaValidationError`. |
| `model.py` | `ModelLoaderService`: manages the lifecycle of a DLH OpenAI-compatible client. `.load()` creates the HTTP client; `.generate(prompt)` calls chat completions; `.extract_sql(output)` pulls SQL from `<begin_sql_response>…<end_sql_response>` tags; `.test_connection()` sends a minimal probe request. `ProviderManager`: registry and lifecycle manager for multiple named providers — `register`, `activate`, `active`, `unload_all`. `create_model_service(settings)` convenience factory. `generate_with_retry(…, retries=2)` wraps `.generate()` with exponential back-off. |
| `processor.py` | `ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph)`: thin orchestration layer between the Streamlit UI and LangGraph. `process_query_with_orchestration(request)` builds an `AgentState`, calls `graph.invoke()`, and maps the final state to a `ComplianceOrchestrationResponse`. `process_query(request)` returns the simpler `ComplianceChatResponse`. Error strings use the what/expected/fix format. |
| `schemas.py` | Pydantic request/response models. `ComplianceChatRequest` (question + optional ID), `ComplianceChatResponse` (simple — no stages), `ComplianceOrchestrationResponse` (full — includes stages audit trail), `LLMConnectionRequest/Response`, `HealthResponse`. Factory helpers: `build_chat_response`, `build_orchestration_response`, `build_error_response`, `build_health_response`, `build_llm_connection_response`. |

### `genai/nl2sql_agent/`

The six LangGraph pipeline nodes and the graph wiring.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `__init__.py` | Re-exports `build_nl2sql_graph`, `create_graph`, `ContextBuilder`, `AgentState`. Documents that no SQLite database is used at runtime. |
| `state.py` | `AgentState` TypedDict (`total=False`). The single mutable dict threaded through all six nodes. Fields: input (`question`, `question_id`), Node 1 outputs (`interpreted_question`, `intent_ok`, `clarification_needed`), Node 2 outputs (`db_path` = Iceberg table name, `schema_context`), Node 3 (`sql_query`), Node 4 (`sql_review_passed`, `review_feedback`, `retry_count`), Node 4.5 (`sqlglot_valid`, `sqlglot_error`), Node 5 (`result_rows`, `result_columns`, `result_summary`), shared (`error`, `stages`). `stages` uses an `Annotated` reducer so LangGraph appends rather than overwrites. |
| `graph.py` | Builds and compiles the LangGraph `StateGraph`. `build_nl2sql_graph(model_service, iceberg_table, connection_name, max_review_retries, sqlglot_dialect)` instantiates all six nodes, registers them, wires every edge and conditional branch, then calls `graph.compile()`. `create_graph(…)` is a convenience wrapper that falls back to `AppSettings` for the table name and connection. |
| `intent_checker.py` | **Node 1.** Rule-based, no LLM. Four sequential checks: empty question → disallowed action words (DELETE/DROP/INSERT/…) → too short with no metric term → vague/ambiguous phrasing. Any failing check returns `intent_ok=False` with a YAML-sourced message. All 24 metric terms (age, kyc, balance, maturity, …) bypass the too-short check. Normalises the question by adding a trailing `?`. |
| `schema_checker.py` | **Node 2.** No LLM, no live database query. Loads Hive column types from `iceberg_schema.txt` at import time. Builds a Markdown schema context block from `databases.toml` (column descriptions, table description) annotated with Iceberg data types. Writes `db_path = "dev_feature_store.COMP_TBL"` into state so downstream nodes know the target table. |
| `sql_writer_agent.py` | **Node 3 — the only LLM call.** `ContextBuilder.build(question, schema_context, feedback)` assembles the full prompt: system role + numbered rules (from `sql_writer.yaml`) + table description + column block + live schema context + few-shot examples (from `databases.toml`) + optional reviewer feedback on retry. Calls `ModelLoaderService.generate(prompt)` then `extract_sql()`. Also contains stateless SQL helpers: `strip_comments`, `normalise_whitespace`, `strip_trailing_semicolon`, `optimise`. |
| `sql_reviewer.py` | **Node 4.** Rule-based, no LLM. Checks in order: retry ceiling guard → empty SQL → disallowed DML/DDL keyword → must start with SELECT → must contain FROM → all referenced table names must exist in `_KNOWN_TABLES` (built from `databases.toml` at import time; handles `db.table` qualified names). On failure increments `retry_count` and returns feedback. On max retries sets `error` and routes to executor. |
| `sql_validator.py` | **Node 4.5.** Uses sqlglot AST parser. Three checks: parse validity (`ParseError` → fail) → statement type must be SELECT → canonicalise to Hive dialect via `stmt.sql(dialect="hive")`. Transparent pass-through if sqlglot is not installed. |
| `executor_agent.py` | **Node 5.** Executes the approved SQL against the Iceberg table via `PandasEngine.read_table()`. Optionally auto-appends `LIMIT <default_limit>` if `enforce_default_limit=true` in `query.toml`. Trims result to `max_display_rows`. Contains standalone formatting helpers: `format_summary`, `to_markdown_table`, `to_csv`, `extract_scalar`. |

### `genai/config/`

Static TOML configuration files. No secrets ever stored here.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `application.toml` | App display name and page title shown in the Streamlit tab. |
| `database.toml` | Database type (`Iceberg`), fully-qualified Iceberg table name (`dev_feature_store.COMP_TBL`), and CML Data connection name. |
| `databases.toml` | **Table registry** — the primary config file for data schema. Registers `COMP_REPORTS.COMP_TBL` with its Iceberg table reference, column business descriptions, filter query template, and few-shot SQL examples. Add new tables here to make them available to the pipeline. |
| `llm.toml` | Default LLM provider, model name, temperature, and max tokens. Overridden by `.env` `MODEL__*` keys. |
| `logging.toml` | Python `logging.config.dictConfig` payload. Configures a console handler (INFO) and a rotating file handler (`data/logs/compliance_bot.log`, 10 MB × 10 backups). |
| `query.toml` | Row-limit policy for the executor: `enforce_default_limit` (bool), `default_limit` (int, default 10), `max_display_rows` (int, default 50). |

### `genai/prompt/`

YAML files that contain every user-facing message and LLM instruction. Edit these to change wording without touching Python code.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `intent_checker.yaml` | Messages shown to the user when intent checks fail: `empty_question`, `disallowed_action`, `too_short`, `clarification`. |
| `schema_checker.yaml` | Section header templates injected into the Markdown schema context: `schema_header`, `table_header`, `col_desc_header`, `sample_header`. |
| `sql_writer.yaml` | System role, numbered rules list, and the full Llama-style `[INST]…[/INST]` prompt template. Also defines `retry.feedback_header` used to label reviewer feedback in retry prompts. |
| `sql_review.yaml` | All feedback strings returned by `SQLReviewer` to the SQL writer on retry, and user-facing error messages for each check failure. |

---

## `utils/`

Shared infrastructure used by every layer.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `__init__.py` | Re-exports all public symbols from the four modules below using relative imports. Single import point for the rest of the codebase. |
| `config.py` | Pydantic `BaseSettings` classes: `DatabaseSettings` (Iceberg table, connection name), `ModelSettings` (provider, model name, URL, API key, SSL flag, temperature, max tokens), `AppSettings` (top-level — merges all nested settings; reads `.env` via `env_nested_delimiter="__"`). `get_settings()` returns a singleton. Also defines shared constants: `SQL_BEGIN_TAG`, `SQL_END_TAG`, `COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME`, `DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS`, `DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE`. |
| `helpers.py` | Stateless stdlib-only utility functions: `safe_json_loads`, `safe_loads`, `safe_dumps`, `extract_json_from_text`, `extract_json_object`, `is_valid_json`, `truncate`, `normalise_whitespace`, `flatten_list`, `pretty_print`, `ensure_directory`, `read_text`, `write_text`, `file_size`, `list_files`, `resolve_project_path`. No external dependencies. |
| `logger.py` | `get_logger(name)` factory. On first call bootstraps the logging system from `genai/config/logging.toml` via `logging.config.dictConfig`. Falls back to `basicConfig` if the TOML is missing. Guards with a module-level `_LOGGING_CONFIGURED` flag so setup runs only once per process. All modules call this instead of `logging.getLogger` directly. |
| `registry.py` | Three concerns in one file: **(1) DB registry** — parses `databases.toml` at import time into an in-memory dict; exposes `get_table_meta`, `get_few_shots`, `get_all_tables`, `list_databases`, `list_tables`, `reload`. **(2) SQLite helpers** — `open_readonly` context manager (BEGIN DEFERRED + unconditional rollback), `fetch_all`, `fetch_scalar`, `table_exists`, `get_column_names`, `row_count`. **(3) YAML prompt cache** — `load_prompt(filename)` parses and caches YAML files from `genai/prompt/`; `get_prompt(filename, *keys)` walks nested keys. |
| `_hdfs_client.py` | `HDFSClient` dataclass. PyArrow-based HDFS interface for file operations. `upload` / `download` use the HDFS CLI (`hdfs dfs`) due to auth constraints. `read_parquet_table` / `read_csv_file` use `pyarrow.dataset`. `load_model_sentence_transformer` / `load_model_cross_encoder` download a model directory from HDFS to a temp dir and load it. |
| `_cml_api.py` | `CAIClient`. Wraps the CML API (`cmlapi`) to create and run CML jobs programmatically. `job_config_template(…)` builds a `CreateJobRequest`; `create_job(config)` submits it; `create_job_run(job_id)` starts execution. Runtime identifiers for Hadoop CLI and Spark 3.5.1 are pinned as constants. |

---

## `docs/`

Project documentation. All files are Markdown.

| File | What it covers |
|---|---|
| `codebase-reference.md` | *This file.* Every file and folder with a concise description. |
| `index.md` | Quick-start, documentation map, project layout tree, key concepts glossary, minimal `.env` example. |
| `architecture.md` | Full system flow diagram, folder tree with annotations, component layers (Presentation → Orchestration → Pipeline → Infrastructure → Storage), LangGraph node graph with all conditional edges, AgentState data-flow table (which node reads/writes which keys), config resolution order. |
| `agents.md` | Deep-dive on each of the six LangGraph nodes: checks performed, outputs written, routing rules, and how to add a new node. |
| `guardrails.md` | All four defence layers: IntentChecker (keyword rules), SQLReviewer (regex + registry), SQLValidator (sqlglot AST), ExecutorAgent (Iceberg read-only connection). |
| `configuration.md` | Every config file and `.env` key. Full `AppSettings` / `DatabaseSettings` / `ModelSettings` class listings. `databases.toml` schema with annotated examples. `query.toml` row-limit policy. |
| `logging.md` | Log configuration reference, output location, handler levels, how to filter logs by agent node prefix. |
| `add-database.md` | Step-by-step guide: register a new Iceberg table in `databases.toml`, add column descriptions and few-shots, verify with `reload_registry()`. |
| `file-connections.md` | Import graph, data-flow map, read/write relationships between every file and folder. |
| `running.md` | Launch instructions for local development, CML Application deployment, and common troubleshooting. |
| `testing.md` | Test strategy, how to run the test suite, and how to write unit tests for new agent nodes. |

---

## `jobs/`

CML Job scripts — run outside the Streamlit app as batch processes.

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `example_job.py` | Template CML job. Demonstrates how to import `CompliancePipeline` and `CAIClient`, submit a batch compliance query programmatically, and log results. |

# Configuration

Configuration is layered. Later sources override earlier ones:

```
genai/config/*.toml   (lowest priority — hardcoded defaults)
        ↓
utils/config.py       (Pydantic Field defaults)
        ↓
      .env             (local overrides — never committed to git)
        ↓
 Environment vars      (highest priority — CI / deployment injection)
        ↓
 llm_config.json       (credentials saved by the Streamlit UI at runtime)
```

---


`.env` is loaded by `pydantic-settings` (`AppSettings` in `utils/config.py`).  
It is **ignored by git** — never commit it.

```dotenv
# Application
APPLICATION_NAME="COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
LOG_LEVEL=INFO          # DEBUG | INFO | WARNING | ERROR
LOG_DIR=data/logs       # always resolved relative to project root

# Database
DATABASE__DB_TYPE=SQLite
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/comp_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=COMP_REPORTS.db

# LLM (DLH on-prem)
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__MODEL_MAX_NEW_TOKENS=512
MODEL__MODEL_TEMPERATURE=0.1
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY         # replace with real key if required
MODEL__DLH_VERIFY_SSL=false
```

---


When you click **Test & Save** on the Streamlit LLM Configuration page, credentials are written to `llm_config.json` in the project root:

```json
{
    "model_name": "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
    "base_url": "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
    "api_key": "EMPTY"
}
```

On next startup `app.py` reads this file and pre-fills the form inputs. This means you do not need to re-enter credentials after a Streamlit restart.

> `llm_config.json` is created in the **project root** (one level above `dlh_ai/`).  
> Add it to `.gitignore` if the key is sensitive.

---


Human-readable display names.

```toml
[application]
name        = "COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
page_config = "SBI COMPLIANCE-BOT"
version     = "3.0.0"
```

---


```toml
[database]
db_type   = "SQLite"
db_folder = "data/comp_db"
db_name   = "COMP_REPORTS.db"
```

`DatabaseSettings.db_path` resolves these to an absolute path:

```python
Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / self.db_folder / self.db_name
```

---


The **table registry** — consumed by `utils/registry.py` and injected into every LLM prompt.

```toml
[databases.<KEY>]
db_file     = "data/<folder>/<FILE>.db"
description = "One sentence about this database."

[databases.<KEY>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>]
description  = "What this table represents."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM <TABLE_NAME> WHERE BRANCH = '{branch}'"

[databases.<KEY>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>.columns]
COL1 = "Business description of column 1"
COL2 = "Business description of column 2"

[databases.<KEY>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: Natural language question.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT ...;
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

See [add-database.md](add-database.md) for the full walkthrough.

---


```toml
[llm]
provider             = "dlh"
model_name           = "google/gemma-4-31b-it"
model_max_new_tokens = 512
model_temperature    = 0.1

[llm.dlh]
base_url   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"
api_key    = "EMPTY"
verify_ssl = false
```

> These are fallback defaults. Override via `.env` (`MODEL__*` keys) or via the Streamlit LLM Configuration page.

---


Controls how many rows the ExecutorAgent fetches.

```toml
[query]
enforce_default_limit = false   # true = auto-append LIMIT if SQL has none
default_limit         = 10      # rows returned when enforce=true
max_display_rows      = 500     # hard ceiling enforced via fetchmany()
```

**When to use `enforce_default_limit = true`:** if users frequently omit `LIMIT` and return very large result sets.

---


See [logging.md](logging.md) for the full reference.

Key fields:

| Key | Default | What to change |
|-----|---------|---------------|
| `handlers.console.level` | `INFO` | Set `DEBUG` to see all agent decisions in terminal |
| `handlers.rotating_file.maxBytes` | `10485760` (10 MB) | Increase if log files fill too fast |
| `handlers.rotating_file.backupCount` | `10` | How many rolled files to keep |

---


```python
class DatabaseSettings(BaseSettings):
    db_type:   str  = "SQLite"
    db_folder: str  = "data/comp_db"
    db_name:   str  = "COMP_REPORTS.db"

    @property
    def db_path(self) -> Path:
        return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / self.db_folder / self.db_name


class ModelSettings(BaseSettings):
    provider:              str   = "dlh"
    model_name:            str   = "google/gemma-4-31b-it"
    model_max_new_tokens:  int   = 512
    model_temperature:     float = 0.1
    dlh_base_url:          str   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"
    dlh_api_key:           str   = "EMPTY"
    dlh_verify_ssl:        bool  = False


class AppSettings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_nested_delimiter="__",   # DATABASE__DB_NAME → database.db_name
    )
    application_name: str  = "COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
    log_level:        str  = "INFO"
    log_dir:          str  = "data/logs"
    host:             str  = "0.0.0.0"
    port:             int  = 8000
    debug:            bool = False
    database: DatabaseSettings = Field(default_factory=DatabaseSettings)
    model:    ModelSettings    = Field(default_factory=ModelSettings)
```

---


```python
SQL_BEGIN_TAG  = "<begin_sql_response>"
SQL_END_TAG    = "<end_sql_response>"

COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME     = "COMP_TBL"     # default table for SQLWriterAgent
BRANCH_CODE_PLACEHOLDER   = "{branch_code}"

DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS    = 512
DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE       = 0.1
```

Change `COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME` if your primary table has a different name.

```
COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH-V3-STREAMLIT/           ← workspace root
│
├── llm_config.json                             ← written by app.py, read by app.py on startup
│
└── dlh_ai/                                     ← Python project root (on sys.path)
    │
    ├── application/
    │   ├── app.py                  ★ ENTRY POINT — only file you run
    │   └── fonts/                  ← Urbanist TTF files, read by app.py at startup
    │
    ├── genai/
    │   ├── config/                 ← static TOML files, read by registry.py / logger.py / executor_agent.py
    │   │   ├── application.toml    ← informational only (not loaded at runtime)
    │   │   ├── database.toml       ← informational only (superseded by DatabaseSettings)
    │   │   ├── databases.toml  ★★★ ← table metadata + few-shots (read by registry.py)
    │   │   ├── llm.toml            ← informational only (superseded by ModelSettings)
    │   │   ├── logging.toml    ★★  ← logger config (read by logger.py)
    │   │   └── query.toml      ★★  ← row-limit policy (read by executor_agent.py)
    │   │
    │   ├── core/
    │   │   ├── exceptions.py       ← base + domain exceptions (imported by model, processor, nodes)
    │   │   ├── schemas.py          ← Pydantic request/response models (imported by app, processor)
    │   │   ├── model.py            ← ModelLoaderService + helpers (imported by app, graph, sql_writer)
    │   │   ├── processor.py        ← ComplianceProcessor (imported by app)
    │   │   └── __init__.py         ← re-exports everything from core
    │   │
    │   ├── nl2sql_agent/
    │   │   ├── state.py            ← AgentState TypedDict (imported by every node + graph + processor)
    │   │   ├── graph.py            ← LangGraph factory (imported by app)
    │   │   ├── intent_checker.py   ← Node 1 (registered in graph)
    │   │   ├── schema_checker.py   ← Node 2 (registered in graph)
    │   │   ├── sql_writer_agent.py ← Node 3 (registered in graph)
    │   │   ├── sql_reviewer.py     ← Node 4 (registered in graph)
    │   │   ├── sql_validator.py    ← Node 4.5 (registered in graph)
    │   │   ├── executor_agent.py   ← Node 5 (registered in graph)
    │   │   └── __init__.py
    │   │
    │   └── prompt/                 ← YAML prompt files (read by registry.py on demand)
    │       ├── intent_checker.yaml ← read by intent_checker.py
    │       ├── schema_checker.yaml ← read by schema_checker.py
    │       ├── sql_writer.yaml     ← read by sql_writer_agent.py
    │       └── sql_review.yaml     ← read by sql_reviewer.py
    │
    ├── utils/
    │   ├── config.py               ← AppSettings / ModelSettings / DatabaseSettings (imported by many)
    │   ├── logger.py               ← get_logger() factory (imported by every module)
    │   ├── registry.py             ← TOML + YAML loaders + SQLite helpers (imported by most agents)
    │   ├── helpers.py              ← stateless stdlib helpers (imported on demand)
    │   └── __init__.py             ← re-exports everything from utils
    │
    ├── data/
    │   ├── comp_db/
    │   │   └── COMP_REPORTS.db     ← SQLite database (read by schema_checker, executor_agent)
    │   ├── logs/
    │   │   └── compliance_bot.log  ← written by every logger, configured in logging.toml
    │   └── seed_synthetic_data.py  ← standalone CLI, writes COMP_REPORTS.db
    │
    ├── docs/                       ← documentation (you are here)
    ├── .env.example                ← copy to .env and fill in credentials
    ├── requirements.txt
    └── pyproject.toml
```

> **Workspace root** also contains `Makefile` — run `make help` for common commands.

---


Read as `A ──imports──► B` meaning file A contains `from B import ...` or `import B`.

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║  ENTRY POINT                                                                     ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

  application/app.py
       │
       ├──imports──► genai/core/model.py          (ModelLoaderService)
       ├──imports──► genai/core/processor.py      (ComplianceProcessor)
       ├──imports──► genai/core/schemas.py        (ComplianceChatRequest)
       ├──imports──► genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py  (build_nl2sql_graph)
       ├──imports──► utils/config.py              (ModelSettings, get_settings)
       └──imports──► utils/logger.py              (get_logger)


╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║  GENAI / CORE                                                                    ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

  genai/core/model.py
       ├──imports──► genai/core/exceptions.py     (ModelLoadError, ModelInferenceError, …)
       ├──imports──► utils/config.py              (ModelSettings, SQL tags, defaults)
       └──imports──► utils/logger.py              (get_logger)

  genai/core/processor.py
       ├──imports──► genai/core/exceptions.py     (ComplianceBotError)

       ├──imports──► genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py  (AgentState)
       └──imports──► utils/logger.py              (get_logger)

  genai/core/schemas.py
       └──imports──► pydantic                     (BaseModel, Field, field_validator)

  genai/core/exceptions.py
       └──(no internal imports — stdlib Exception only)

  genai/core/__init__.py
       ├──imports──► genai/core/exceptions.py     (all exception classes)
       ├──imports──► genai/core/model.py          (ModelLoaderService, create_model_service)
       ├──imports──► genai/core/processor.py      (ComplianceProcessor)
       ├──imports──► genai/core/schemas.py        (all response models)
       └──imports──► utils/config.py              (AppSettings, DatabaseSettings, ModelSettings)


╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
```
