       ├──imports──► utils/helpers.py                 (all helper functions)
       ├──imports──► utils/logger.py                  (get_logger)
       └──imports──► utils/registry.py                (all registry functions)
```

---


| File | Imports from | Imported by |
|------|-------------|-------------|
| `application/app.py` | `genai/core/model`, `genai/core/processor`, `genai/core/schemas`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph`, `utils/config`, `utils/logger` | — *(entry point, nothing imports it)* |
| `genai/core/exceptions.py` | *(nothing internal)* | `genai/core/model`, `genai/core/processor`, `genai/core/__init__` |
| `genai/core/schemas.py` | pydantic | `genai/core/processor`, `genai/core/__init__`, `application/app.py` (inline) |
| `genai/core/model.py` | `genai/core/exceptions`, `utils/config`, `utils/logger` | `application/app.py`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent`, `genai/core/__init__` |
| `genai/core/processor.py` | `genai/core/exceptions`, `genai/core/schemas`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/logger` | `application/app.py`, `genai/core/__init__` |
| `genai/core/__init__.py` | `genai/core/*`, `utils/config` | *(optional convenience import)* |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | stdlib typing + operator | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph`, every node file, `genai/core/processor` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | all 6 node files, `genai/core/model`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/config`, `utils/logger` | `application/app.py` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/logger`, `utils/registry` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/logger`, `utils/registry` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py` | `genai/core/model`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/config`, `utils/logger`, `utils/registry` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/logger`, `utils/registry` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/logger`, (sqlglot lazy) | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/state`, `utils/logger`, (tomllib lazy) | `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph` |
| `utils/config.py` | pydantic, pydantic_settings | `application/app.py`, `genai/core/model`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph`, `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent`, `genai/core/__init__`, `utils/__init__` |
| `utils/logger.py` | stdlib logging, pathlib, (tomllib lazy) | **every Python file** via `get_logger()` |
| `utils/registry.py` | `utils/logger`, yaml, (tomllib lazy) | `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker`, `schema_checker`, `sql_writer_agent`, `sql_reviewer`, `utils/__init__` |
| `utils/helpers.py` | stdlib only | `utils/__init__` |
| `utils/__init__.py` | `utils/config`, `utils/helpers`, `utils/logger`, `utils/registry` | *(convenience — callers can import from `utils` directly)* |
| `genai/config/databases.toml` | *(TOML data file)* | `utils/registry.py` → `_build_db_registry()` |
| `genai/config/logging.toml` | *(TOML data file)* | `utils/logger.py` → `_load_toml_logging_config()` |
| `genai/config/query.toml` | *(TOML data file)* | `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` → `_load_query_config()` |
| `genai/config/application.toml` | *(TOML data file)* | *(informational — not directly loaded at runtime)* |
| `genai/config/database.toml` | *(TOML data file)* | *(informational — superseded by DatabaseSettings in utils/config.py)* |
| `genai/config/llm.toml` | *(TOML data file)* | *(informational — superseded by ModelSettings in utils/config.py)* |
| `genai/prompt/intent_checker.yaml` | *(YAML data file)* | `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py` via `utils/registry.get_prompt()` |
| `genai/prompt/schema_checker.yaml` | *(YAML data file)* | `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` via `utils/registry.load_prompt()` |
| `genai/prompt/sql_writer.yaml` | *(YAML data file)* | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py` via `utils/registry.load_prompt()` |
| `genai/prompt/sql_review.yaml` | *(YAML data file)* | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py` via `utils/registry.get_prompt()` |
| `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` | *(SQLite database)* | `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` (PRAGMA), `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` (SELECT) |
| `data/logs/compliance_bot.log` | *(log file)* | written by every module via the rotating file handler in `logging.toml` |
| `data/seed_synthetic_data.py` | stdlib only | *(standalone CLI — not imported by any application code)* |
| `llm_config.json` (workspace root) | *(JSON data file)* | written/read by `application/app.py` only |

---


```
  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  application/app.py                                                   │
  │                                                                       │
  │  1. Reads  llm_config.json          (load_config_from_disk)          │
  │  2. Reads  application/fonts/*.ttf  (get_font_base64)                │
  │  3. Builds ModelLoaderService ───► genai/core/model.py               │
  │  4. Builds compiled_graph     ───► genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py       │
  │  5. Wraps  ComplianceProcessor───► genai/core/processor.py           │
  │                                                                       │
  │  On chat submit:                                                      │
  │  6. Creates ComplianceChatRequest  ─► genai/core/schemas.py          │
  │  7. Calls  processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)        │
  └───────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  genai/core/processor.py — ComplianceProcessor                       │
  │                                                                       │
  │  8. Builds initial AgentState ─────► genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py     │
  │  9. Calls  compiled_graph.invoke(initial_state)                       │
  └───────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          │  AgentState flows through LangGraph
                          ▼
  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py — Compiled StateGraph                   │
  │                                                                       │
  │  Node 1  intent_checker.py                                            │
  │    reads:  utils/registry.py ──► genai/prompt/intent_checker.yaml    │
  │    reads:  utils/logger.py                                            │
  │    writes: interpreted_question, intent_ok, clarification_needed      │
  │            stages += ["intent_checker:ok"]                            │
  │                   │                                                   │
  │  Node 2  schema_checker.py                                            │
  │    reads:  data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db   (PRAGMA table_info)        │
  │    reads:  utils/registry.py ──► genai/config/databases.toml         │
  │    reads:  utils/registry.py ──► genai/prompt/schema_checker.yaml    │
  │    writes: db_path, schema_context                                    │
  │            stages += ["schema_checker:db=COMP_REPORTS.db"]            │
  │                   │                                                   │
  │  Node 3  sql_writer_agent.py  ★ ONLY LLM CALL                       │
  │    reads:  utils/registry.py ──► genai/config/databases.toml         │
  │    reads:  utils/registry.py ──► genai/prompt/sql_writer.yaml        │
  │    calls:  genai/core/model.py → ModelLoaderService.generate()        │
  │            → DLH inference server (HTTP)                              │
  │    calls:  genai/core/model.py → extract_sql()                        │
  │    writes: sql_query                                                  │
  │            stages += ["sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0"]       │
  │                   │                                                   │
  │  Node 4  sql_reviewer.py                                              │
  │    reads:  utils/registry.py ──► genai/prompt/sql_review.yaml        │
  │    reads:  data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db   (table existence check)    │
  │    writes: sql_review_passed, review_feedback, retry_count            │
  │            stages += ["sql_reviewer:passed"]                          │
  │                   │                                                   │
  │  Node 4½ sql_validator.py                                             │
  │    uses:   sqlglot (lazy import)                                      │
  │    writes: sqlglot_valid, sqlglot_error, sql_query (canonical)        │
  │            stages += ["sql_validator:passed dialect=sqlite"]          │
  │                   │                                                   │
  │  Node 5  executor_agent.py                                            │
  │    reads:  genai/config/query.toml        (row-limit policy)          │
  │    reads:  data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db   (BEGIN DEFERRED SELECT)    │
  │    writes: result_rows, result_columns, result_summary                │
  │            stages += ["executor_agent:ok rows=N"]                     │
  └───────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          │  Final AgentState returned
                          ▼
  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  genai/core/processor.py                                              │
  │  10. Maps AgentState → ComplianceOrchestrationResponse                │
  │      (genai/core/schemas.py)                                          │
  └───────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  application/app.py                                                   │
  │  11. Renders stage pills, SQL block, result data, CSV download        │
  │  12. Writes llm_config.json if user saved new credentials             │
  └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Throughout all steps:
    Every module ──► utils/logger.py ──► data/logs/compliance_bot.log
```

---



| File | Loaded by | What it controls |
|------|-----------|-----------------|
| `databases.toml` | `utils/registry.py` → `_build_db_registry()` | Table names, column descriptions, few-shot SQL examples, DB file mapping |
| `logging.toml` | `utils/logger.py` → `_load_toml_logging_config()` | Log handlers, formatters, levels, rotating file path |
| `query.toml` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` → `_load_query_config()` | `enforce_default_limit`, `default_limit`, `max_display_rows` |
| `application.toml` | *(informational only)* | App display name — not imported at runtime |
| `database.toml` | *(informational only)* | Superseded by `DatabaseSettings` in `utils/config.py` |
| `llm.toml` | *(informational only)* | Superseded by `ModelSettings` in `utils/config.py` |


| File | Loaded by | Used for |
|------|-----------|---------|
| `intent_checker.yaml` | `intent_checker.py` via `registry.get_prompt()` | User-facing messages: empty, disallowed, too_short, clarification |
| `schema_checker.yaml` | `schema_checker.py` via `registry.load_prompt()` | Header templates: schema_header, table_header, sample_header, error messages |
| `sql_writer.yaml` | `sql_writer_agent.py` via `registry.load_prompt()` | System role, generation rules, prompt template, retry feedback header |
| `sql_review.yaml` | `sql_reviewer.py` via `registry.get_prompt()` | Reviewer feedback strings: max_retries, empty_sql, disallowed_kw, unknown_tables |

  data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db

  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

| File / Resource | Reads it | Writes it | Notes |
|-----------------|----------|-----------|-------|
| `application/app.py` | Streamlit server at startup | — | Entry point — run with `streamlit run` |
| `genai/core/exceptions.py` | `model.py`, `processor.py`, `core/__init__` | — | Never modified at runtime |
| `genai/core/schemas.py` | `processor.py`, `app.py` | — | Pydantic models — no side effects |
| `genai/core/model.py` | `app.py`, `graph.py`, `sql_writer_agent.py` | — | Holds the live HTTP client |
| `genai/core/processor.py` | `app.py` | — | Holds the live compiled graph |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | Every node + `graph.py` + `processor.py` | — | TypedDict only — no side effects |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | `app.py` | — | Returns compiled graph at startup |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py` | `graph.py` | — | |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` | `graph.py` | — | Also reads `.db` files |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py` | `graph.py` | — | Calls DLH LLM over HTTP |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py` | `graph.py` | — | Also reads `.db` for table check |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py` | `graph.py` | — | Uses sqlglot (lazy import) |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` | `graph.py` | — | Reads DB + reads `query.toml` |
| `utils/config.py` | Many modules + `utils/__init__` | — | Singleton — read once at import time |
| `utils/logger.py` | Every Python module | `data/logs/*.log` | Configures once; re-used per-module |
| `utils/registry.py` | Agent nodes + `utils/__init__` | — | In-memory cache for TOML + YAML |
| `utils/helpers.py` | `utils/__init__` | — | Stateless pure functions |
| `utils/__init__.py` | Any module that does `from utils import …` | — | Re-export convenience layer |
| `genai/config/databases.toml` | `utils/registry.py` (cached) | — | Edit to add/change tables |
| `genai/config/logging.toml` | `utils/logger.py` (once) | — | Edit to change log levels or handlers |
| `genai/config/query.toml` | `executor_agent.py` (once) | — | Edit to change row limits |
| `genai/prompt/intent_checker.yaml` | `intent_checker.py` (cached) | — | Edit to change user-facing messages |
| `genai/prompt/schema_checker.yaml` | `schema_checker.py` (cached) | — | Edit to change schema section headers |
| `genai/prompt/sql_writer.yaml` | `sql_writer_agent.py` (cached) | — | Edit to change LLM system prompt |
| `genai/prompt/sql_review.yaml` | `sql_reviewer.py` (cached) | — | Edit to change reviewer feedback strings |
| `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` | `schema_checker.py`, `sql_reviewer.py`, `executor_agent.py`, `utils/registry.py` | `data/seed_synthetic_data.py` | Read-only at runtime; seed script writes it |
| `data/logs/compliance_bot.log` | Developer / admin | Every Python module | Rotating — max 100 MB total |
| `data/seed_synthetic_data.py` | — | `COMP_REPORTS.db` | Standalone CLI; run once to create DB |
| `llm_config.json` | `application/app.py` | `application/app.py` | Credentials — never commit to git |
| `application/fonts/*.ttf` | `application/app.py` | — | Embedded as base64 in page CSS |

####################################################################################################




# Guardrails

The pipeline has **four independent guard layers**.  None of them call the LLM — they are all rule-based and therefore fast, deterministic, and zero-cost.

```
User question
      │
      ▼
 ┌─────────────────────────────┐
 │  Layer 1: IntentChecker     │  Blocks empty / harmful / vague input
 └──────────────┬──────────────┘
                │ passes
                ▼
 ┌─────────────────────────────┐
 │  LLM generates SQL          │
 └──────────────┬──────────────┘
                │
                ▼
 ┌─────────────────────────────┐
 │  Layer 2: SQLReviewer       │  Blocks DML, checks structure + tables
 └──────────────┬──────────────┘
                │ passes
                ▼
 ┌─────────────────────────────┐
 │  Layer 3: SQLValidator      │  AST parse, SELECT-only gate
 └──────────────┬──────────────┘
                │ passes
                ▼
 ┌─────────────────────────────┐
 │  Layer 4: ExecutorAgent     │  BEGIN DEFERRED + unconditional ROLLBACK
 └──────────────┬──────────────┘
                │
                ▼
          Result rows
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py`  
**When:** Before anything reaches the LLM.

### Blocked inputs

| Type | Examples | Response |
|------|----------|----------|
| Empty question | `""`, `"   "` | "No question was provided…" |
| Disallowed action word | `"delete accounts"`, `"drop table"` | "⚠ Sorry, the keyword DELETE is not permitted…" |
| Too short / meaningless | `"pending"`, `"what is"` | "Your question is too short or unclear…" |
| Vague / ambiguous | `"give me data"`, `"please verify this"` | "Your question is unclear. Please rephrase…" |


```
delete  drop  insert  update  truncate  alter  create  replace
pragma  attach  detach  vacuum  modify  remove  erase  wipe  purge  destroy
```


All user-facing strings live in `genai/prompt/intent_checker.yaml`.  Edit that file to change wording — no Python change required.

```yaml
messages:
  empty_question:      "No question was provided…"
  disallowed_action:   "⚠ Sorry, the keyword {blocked_word} is not permitted…"
  too_short:           "Your question is too short or unclear to process."
  clarification:       "Your question is unclear. Please rephrase with more detail."
```


Edit `_METRIC_TERMS` in `intent_checker.py` to add domain-specific terms that should bypass the too-short check:

```python
_METRIC_TERMS: Final[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({
    "age", "kyc", "balance", "maturity", "product", "branch", "circle",
    # ← add your terms here, lowercase
})
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py`  
**When:** After the LLM generates SQL.

### Checks

| Check | Trigger | Action |
|-------|---------|--------|
| Retry ceiling | `retry_count >= max_retries` (default 3) | Sets `error` field → Executor surfaces the message to user |
| Empty SQL | `sql_query == ""` | `retry_count += 1` → back to SQLWriter |
| Disallowed keyword | Any of INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/DROP/TRUNCATE/ALTER/CREATE/REPLACE/PRAGMA/ATTACH/DETACH/VACUUM | `retry_count += 1` → back to SQLWriter |
| No SELECT prefix | SQL doesn't start with `SELECT` | `retry_count += 1` → back to SQLWriter |
| Missing FROM | No `FROM` keyword found | `retry_count += 1` → back to SQLWriter |
| Unknown table | Any table in `FROM`/`JOIN` clauses doesn't exist in the `.db` | `retry_count += 1` → back to SQLWriter |


In `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py`:

```python
compiled_graph = build_nl2sql_graph(
    model_service=model_service,
    db_path=db_path,
    max_review_retries=5,   # ← change here (default: 3)
)
```


All reviewer feedback strings live in `genai/prompt/sql_review.yaml`:

```yaml
feedback:
  empty_sql:        "The SQL Writer returned an empty query."
  no_select:        "The query must begin with SELECT."
  missing_from:     "The query is missing a FROM clause."
  disallowed_kw:    "Keyword {kw} is not allowed in compliance queries."
  unknown_tables:   "The following tables do not exist: {tables}."
  max_retries:      "⚠ The request could not be completed after {max_retries} attempts."
```

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py`  
**When:** After SQLReviewer passes.

### Checks

| Check | What it catches | Example |
|-------|----------------|---------|
| **Parse validity** | Syntax errors the regex reviewer missed | `SELECT * FORM tbl` (typo) |
| **Statement type** | Non-SELECT statements that regex missed | `EXPLAIN SELECT …` (type = `Command`) |
| **Dialect normalisation** | Vendor-specific syntax not valid in SQLite | Rewrites to canonical SQLite |

### Changing dialect

In `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py`:

```python
compiled_graph = build_nl2sql_graph(
    ...
    sqlglot_dialect="sqlite",   # ← change here if you use a different DB engine
)
```


If sqlglot is not installed the node automatically skips and sets `sqlglot_valid=True`.

---


**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py`  
**When:** After all prior layers pass.


```python
conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")   # No write locks acquired
cursor = conn.execute(sql)       # SELECT only reaches here
# ...
conn.rollback()                  # Always — even if execute() succeeded
conn.close()
```

`BEGIN DEFERRED` means SQLite acquires no lock at all until the first read.  Because we always rollback, **no write can ever be committed** regardless of what SQL text reaches the executor.

### Row-limit policy

Controlled entirely by `genai/config/query.toml`:

```toml
[query]
enforce_default_limit = false   # true = auto-append LIMIT if SQL has none
default_limit         = 10      # rows appended when enforce=true
max_display_rows      = 500     # hard ceiling via fetchmany()
```

`max_display_rows` applies regardless of `enforce_default_limit` — it is a hard memory/performance ceiling enforced by `cursor.fetchmany(max_display_rows)`.

---


| Attack / mistake | Layer 1 | Layer 2 | Layer 3 | Layer 4 |
|-----------------|---------|---------|---------|---------|
| `DELETE FROM …` | ✓ (action word) | ✓ (keyword check) | ✓ (non-SELECT AST) | ✓ (rollback) |
| `SELECT 1; DROP TABLE …` | — | ✓ (DROP keyword) | ✓ (multiple stmts) | ✓ (rollback) |
| SQL with typo | — | — | ✓ (ParseError) | — |
| SQL with wrong table | — | ✓ (table check) | — | — |
| Vague non-query | ✓ (vague check) | — | — | — |
| 10 MB result set | — | — | — | ✓ (fetchmany cap) |


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\docs\index.md
####################################################################################################





> **Version 3.0.0 (Streamlit)** · LangGraph-powered NL-to-SQL compliance query engine for banking term-deposit products.  
> The UI is served entirely by **Streamlit** — no separate backend server or proxy required.

---

## Quick Start

```bash
pip install -r dlh_ai/requirements.txt

python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000

streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py
```

Open **http://localhost:8501** in a browser.

| **[configuration.md](configuration.md)** | Every config file and `.env` key explained |
| **[running.md](running.md)** | Launch instructions and troubleshooting |

---

## Project Layout

```
COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH-V3-STREAMLIT/
├── dlh_ai/
│   ├── application/
│   │   ├── app.py           ← Streamlit app (single entry-point)
│   │   └── fonts/           ← Urbanist TTF files (base64 embedded)
│   │
│   ├── genai/
│   │   ├── config/          ← TOML configuration files (no secrets)
│   │   ├── core/            ← exceptions, schemas, model service, processor
│   │   ├── nl2sql_agent/    ← six LangGraph pipeline nodes
│   │   └── prompt/          ← YAML prompt files (one per agent node)
│   │
│   ├── utils/               ← shared helpers, config loader, registry, logger
│   ├── data/
│   │   ├── comp_db/         ← SQLite database files
│   │   ├── logs/            ← rotating log files
│   │   └── seed_synthetic_data.py
│   │
│   ├── docs/                ← ← you are here
│   └── requirements.txt
│
└── llm_config.json          ← persisted LLM credentials (auto-created by app)
```

---

## Key Concepts

| Term | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **AgentState** | Single `TypedDict` that flows through every node; each node writes only its own keys |
| **LangGraph** | Directed state-graph framework; nodes are Python callables, edges are conditional routes |
| **NL2SQL** | Natural Language to SQL — translating a user question into a SQLite SELECT statement |
| **databases.toml** | The single file where you register new databases and tables |
| **YAML prompts** | All LLM instructions live in `genai/prompt/*.yaml`; no strings are hardcoded in Python |
| **DLH** | On-prem LLM inference server (OpenAI-compatible API); configured in `.env` or the UI |
| **llm_config.json** | Credentials saved by the Streamlit LLM Configuration page (project root) |

---

## Minimal `.env`

```dotenv
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://<your-dlh-host>:9000/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/comp_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=COMP_REPORTS.db
```

See **[configuration.md](configuration.md)** for the full reference.


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\docs\logging.md
####################################################################################################




# Logging

## Overview

All logging is bootstrapped through a single factory function:

```python
from utils.logger import get_logger
LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.my_module")
```

**First call** configures the entire Python logging system from `genai/config/logging.toml`.  
Subsequent calls just return the named logger from the standard library cache.

---

## Log Destinations

| Handler | Level | Where | Format |
|---------|-------|-------|--------|
| `console` | INFO | stdout | `asctime \| LEVEL \| name \| module:lineno \| message` |
| `rotating_file` | DEBUG | `data/logs/compliance_bot.log` | same |

The file handler uses `RotatingFileHandler` — it rolls over at **10 MB** and keeps **10 backups**.

---

## Log File Location

The log directory is always `<project_root>/dlh_ai/data/logs/` regardless of which directory you run the app from.

This is enforced in `utils/logger.py`:

```python
_PROJECT_ROOT: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
# ...
resolved_log_dir = raw_dir if raw_dir.is_absolute() else _PROJECT_ROOT / raw_dir
```

So even if Streamlit is started from a different working directory, the log file always ends up at:

```
dlh_ai/data/logs/compliance_bot.log
```

---


`genai/config/logging.toml` — edit this to change log behaviour.

```toml
[logging]
version                  = 1
disable_existing_loggers = false

[logging.formatters.standard]
format  = "%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(module)s:%(lineno)d | %(message)s"
datefmt = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"

[logging.handlers.console]
class     = "logging.StreamHandler"
level     = "INFO"       # ← change to DEBUG to see all messages in terminal
formatter = "standard"
stream    = "ext://sys.stdout"

[logging.handlers.rotating_file]
class       = "logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler"
level       = "DEBUG"    # ← file always gets DEBUG and above
formatter   = "standard"
filename    = "data/logs/compliance_bot.log"  # overwritten at runtime with absolute path
maxBytes    = 10485760   # 10 MB
backupCount = 10
encoding    = "utf-8"
delay       = true       # don't create the file until first write

[logging.loggers.compliance_bot]
level     = "DEBUG"
handlers  = ["console", "rotating_file"]
propagate = false        # ← don't bubble to the root logger

[logging.root]
level    = "WARNING"
handlers = ["console"]
```

---


**Option 1 — `.env`:**
```dotenv
LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG
```

**Option 2 — TOML:**
```toml
[logging.handlers.console]
level = "DEBUG"    # was "INFO"
```

**Option 3 — Python (runtime, targeted):**
```python
import logging
logging.getLogger("compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.sql_writer_agent").setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
```

---

## Logger Hierarchy

All loggers follow the dot-separated hierarchy:

```
compliance_bot                                  ← root for all app loggers
compliance_bot.streamlit                        app.py (Streamlit entry-point)
compliance_bot.core.model                       model.py
compliance_bot.core.processor                   processor.py
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.graph               graph.py
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.intent_checker
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.schema_checker
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.sql_writer_agent
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.sql_reviewer
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.sql_validator
compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.executor_agent
compliance_bot.utils.registry
```

Because `compliance_bot` is configured in `logging.toml` with `propagate = false`, all of these are captured by the `rotating_file` and `console` handlers automatically — you never need to configure child loggers individually.

---

## Reading Log Lines

```
2026-08-07 22:43:24 | INFO     | compliance_bot.nl2sql_agent.graph | graph:166 | NL2SQL LangGraph compiled successfully.
│                      │           │                                  │            │
│                      │           logger name                        file:line   message
│                      level
timestamp
```


Each agent node appends a string to the `stages` list. After a request completes you'll see:

```
pipeline complete — stages=['intent_checker:ok',
                             'schema_checker:db=COMP_REPORTS.db',
                             'sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0',
                             'sql_reviewer:passed',
                             'sql_validator:passed dialect=sqlite',
                             'executor_agent:ok rows=12']
```

This is logged at `INFO` level by `ComplianceProcessor._map_state_to_response()`.

---


```python
from utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.my_new_module")

LOGGER.info("Processing request: question=%r", question)
LOGGER.warning("Retrying — attempt %d/%d", attempt, max_retries)
LOGGER.exception("Unexpected error in %s", context)   # includes traceback
```

> **Do not use f-strings in log calls.** Use `%s` / `%r` / `%d` so the message is only formatted when the log level is actually enabled.

FILE: dlh_ai\docs\running.md

> **Golden rule:** Always run commands from the **workspace root** (`COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH-V3-STREAMLIT\`).

---

## Prerequisites

```bash
pip install -r dlh_ai/requirements.txt

python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 2000
```

---


A `Makefile` at the workspace root provides common commands:

```bash
make run        # launch Streamlit app
make seed       # seed / re-seed the SQLite database
make lint       # run ruff linter
make typecheck  # run mypy type-checker
make clean      # remove __pycache__ and .pyc files
make help       # list all available targets
```

---


```bash
streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py
```

The browser opens at **http://localhost:8501** automatically.

**Custom port:**
```bash
streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py --server.port 8502
```

**Bind to all interfaces (e.g. CML / cloud):**
```bash
streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py --server.address 0.0.0.0 --server.port 8501
```

**Stopping:** `Ctrl+C`

---


```bash
python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py

python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000

python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 0

python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 500 --seed 123
```

Output file: `dlh_ai/data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db`

---

## Quick Reference

| Goal | Command (from workspace root) |
|------|-----------------------------|
| Start app | `streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py` |
| Start on custom port | `streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py --server.port 8502` |
| Seed database | `python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 2000` |
| Reset and re-seed | `python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000` |

---

## Troubleshooting


```powershell
# Find the process
netstat -ano | findstr :8501

# Kill it
taskkill /F /PID <pid>
```

Or just use a different port:
```bash
streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py --server.port 8502
```


Run from the **workspace root**, not from inside `application/` or `dlh_ai/`:

```bash
# ✗ wrong
cd dlh_ai/application
streamlit run app.py

# ✓ correct
streamlit run dlh_ai/application/app.py
```


The LangGraph pipeline is not yet initialised. In the Streamlit UI:
1. Navigate to **LLM Configuration** in the sidebar.
2. Enter credentials and click **Test & Save**.


1. Verify `MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL` in `.env` (or the URL you typed in the UI).
2. Confirm the DLH inference server is reachable:
   ```bash
   curl http://<dlh-host>:9000/api/v1/models
   ```
3. Check the API key — even `EMPTY` must exactly match what the server expects.


```bash
python dlh_ai/data/seed_synthetic_data.py
```

This creates `dlh_ai/data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db`.

---

## Logging

All logs are written to `dlh_ai/data/logs/compliance_bot.log` (rotating, 10 MB × 10 files).

To increase verbosity add to `.env`:
```dotenv
LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG
```

Or edit `dlh_ai/genai/config/logging.toml`:
```toml
[logging.handlers.console]
level = "DEBUG"
```

See [logging.md](logging.md) for the full reference.

# Testing

> The test suite has been **removed** from this version of the project.  
> The codebase is production-ready for the Streamlit deployment.  
> All pipeline logic (agent nodes, registry, helpers) is covered by inline type annotations, docstrings, and the Pydantic schema validation that runs at request time.

If you wish to add tests in future, here is how the test layer was structured.

---


Add pytest and mock dependencies (not in `requirements.txt` for the Streamlit build):

```bash
pip install pytest pytest-mock pytest-cov
```

Create a `dlh_ai/tests/` folder with a `conftest.py`:

```python
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def tmp_db(tmp_path_factory) -> Path:
    """Temporary SQLite DB with 5 seeded COMP_TBL rows."""
    db_path = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("db") / "test.db"
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    conn.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE COMP_TBL (
            BRCD TEXT, CUST_NBR TEXT, AGE_YRS INTEGER,
            KYC_FLG TEXT, CIRCLENAME TEXT, SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG INTEGER
        )
    """)
    conn.executemany(
        "INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
        [("001","C1",65,"Y","DELHI",1),
         ("002","C2",45,"N","MUMBAI METRO",0),
         ("003","C3",72,"Y","MUMBAI METRO",1),
         ("004","C4",33,"N","CHENNAI",0),
         ("005","C5",61,"Y","DELHI",1)],
    )
    conn.commit(); conn.close()
    return db_path
```

---


| File | Module tested |
|------|--------------|
| `test_utils_helpers.py` | `utils/helpers.py` — JSON, I/O, truncate |
| `test_utils_config.py` | `utils/config.py` — AppSettings, ModelSettings |
| `test_utils_logger.py` | `utils/logger.py` — `get_logger`, flag |
| `test_utils_registry.py` | `utils/registry.py` — `load_prompt`, cache |
| `test_exceptions.py` | `genai/core/exceptions.py` — exception hierarchy |
| `test_schemas.py` | `genai/core/schemas.py` — Pydantic models |
| `test_intent_checker.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py` |
| `test_sql_validator.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py` |
| `test_sql_reviewer.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py` |
| `test_executor_agent.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` |
| `test_schema_checker.py` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` |

---

## Example Test

```python
from genai.nl2sql_agent.intent_checker import IntentChecker
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState

class TestIntentCheckerHappyPath:
    def test_valid_question(self):
        node = IntentChecker()
        result = node.run(AgentState(question="How many accounts have KYC flag N?"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True
        assert result["clarification_needed"] is None

class TestIntentCheckerDisallowed:
    def test_delete_blocked(self):
        node = IntentChecker()
        result = node.run(AgentState(question="Delete all accounts"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert result["clarification_needed"] is not None
```

---


```toml
[tool.pytest.ini_options]
testpaths         = ["dlh_ai/tests"]
addopts           = "--strict-markers -v --tb=short --import-mode=importlib"
pythonpath        = ["dlh_ai"]
```

---


| Module | Reason |
|--------|--------|
| `genai/core/model.py` — `ModelLoaderService.generate()` | Requires a live DLH inference server |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py` — `SQLWriterAgent.run()` | Requires a live LLM |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` — end-to-end `graph.invoke()` | Requires a live LLM |
| `application/app.py` — Streamlit UI | Requires a live Streamlit session |


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\docs\.ipynb_checkpoints\add-database-checkpoint.md
####################################################################################################





This page walks you through connecting a second (or third) SQLite database to the pipeline with **minimal code changes** — the architecture is designed so that adding a database is almost entirely a configuration task.

**Total files to edit: 2** (plus your `.db` file itself).

---

## Overview

The pipeline discovers databases at runtime by:

1. `SchemaChecker` scanning the `data_root` folder for `*.db` files and scoring each one against the question.
2. `SQLWriterAgent` reading table metadata (descriptions, column definitions, few-shots) from `genai/config/databases.toml`.

You do **not** need to touch any Python files.

---


Put the SQLite database file anywhere under `dlh_ai/data/`:

```
dlh_ai/data/
├── comp_db/
│   └── COMP_REPORTS.db          ← existing
└── my_new_db/
    └── MY_NEW_DATABASE.db       ← new file
```

> The exact sub-folder name does not matter — `SchemaChecker` uses `data_root.glob("**/*.db")`.

If you need to create the database from a CSV or schema script, run that first and place the `.db` file here.

---


Open `dlh_ai/genai/config/databases.toml` and add a new `[databases.<key>]` block:

```toml
[databases.COMP_REPORTS]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db"
description = "Banking compliance reports database for term-deposit products."

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL]
description  = "..."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE ACCT_BRNCH IN ({branch_code})"

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL.columns]
BRCD = "BRANCH CODE OR BRANCH NUMBER"


[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE]
db_file     = "data/my_new_db/MY_NEW_DATABASE.db"
description = "Short description of what this database contains."

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE]
description  = "What this table represents."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE BRANCH_ID = '{branch_id}'"

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE.columns]
BRANCH_ID    = "Unique identifier for the branch"
CUSTOMER_ID  = "Unique customer identifier"
PRODUCT_TYPE = "Type of financial product"
BALANCE      = "Current account balance in INR"
STATUS       = "Account status: ACTIVE, CLOSED, DORMANT"

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: Show all active accounts in MY_TABLE.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE';
<end_sql_response>

Question: Count accounts by product type.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT PRODUCT_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM MY_TABLE GROUP BY PRODUCT_TYPE ORDER BY cnt DESC;
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

### Field reference

| Field | Required | Description |
|-------|----------|-------------|
| `db_file` | ✓ | Relative path to the `.db` file from the **project root** (`dlh_ai/`) |
| `description` | recommended | Injected into the LLM prompt as context |
| `tables.<NAME>.description` | recommended | Per-table description in the LLM prompt |
| `tables.<NAME>.filter_query` | optional | Template for branch-filtered queries |
| `tables.<NAME>.columns.<COL>` | recommended | Business description of each column |
| `tables.<NAME>.few_shots.examples` | strongly recommended | NL→SQL examples for this table |

---


By default the app uses the database configured under `DATABASE__DB_FOLDER` / `DATABASE__DB_NAME` in `.env` (or `AppSettings` defaults). If you want **this** new database to be the default:

```dotenv
# .env
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/my_new_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=MY_NEW_DATABASE.db
```

If you want to keep `COMP_REPORTS.db` as the default but also support the new one, **no `.env` change is needed** — `SchemaChecker` automatically routes questions to the best-matching database based on keyword overlap.

---

## Step 4 — Verify

```python
python -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'dlh_ai')
from utils.registry import _build_db_registry
reg = _build_db_registry()
print(list(reg.keys()))
"
```

You should see `MY_TABLE` (or whatever your table name is) alongside `COMP_TBL`.

---

## Step 5 — Test

Restart Streamlit, then ask a question specific to the new table and check the **Agent Trace** in the result — you should see `schema_checker:db=MY_NEW_DATABASE.db`.

---

## Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |
|---------|-------------|-----|
| SchemaChecker still uses old DB | Keyword scores tied | Add more specific question keywords in `few_shots.examples` |
| `MY_TABLE` not in registry | Typo in `databases.toml` table key | Key must exactly match the SQLite table name |
| `FileNotFoundError` in registry | Wrong `db_file` path | Path is relative to `dlh_ai/` project root, not to `genai/config/` |
| SQL always wrong for new table | No few-shots | Add at least 3 diverse `few_shots.examples` |
| Column names rejected by reviewer | Table not in `sqlite_master` | Ensure the `.db` file exists and the table was created |

---

## Summary

| # | File | What to add |
|---|------|-------------|
| 1 | `dlh_ai/data/<folder>/<NAME>.db` | The SQLite database file itself |
| 2 | `dlh_ai/genai/config/databases.toml` | One `[databases.<KEY>]` block per database, one `[tables.<NAME>]` block per table |

That's it. No Python changes required.

---


If your new table should be the **only** table the pipeline ever queries:

1. Set `DATABASE__DB_NAME` in `.env` to your new `.db` file.
2. Set `COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME` in `dlh_ai/utils/config.py`:

```python
COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME: str = "MY_TABLE"   # was "COMP_TBL"
```

3. Update `SQLWriterAgent` default in `dlh_ai/genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py`:

```python
class SQLWriterAgent:
    def __init__(
        self,
        model_service: ModelLoaderService,
        table_name: str = "MY_TABLE",    # was COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME
    ) -> None:
```


####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\genai\__init__.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai — Enterprise GenAI package for the Compliance Chatbot.

Quickstart (recommended)
------------------------
    from dlh_ai.genai.pipeline import CompliancePipeline

    pipeline = CompliancePipeline(
        model_name = "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
        base_url   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
        api_key    = "EMPTY",
    )

    result = pipeline.ask("List accounts where KYC flag is N")

    if result.ok:
        print(result.summary)
        display(result.table)
    else:
        print(result.error_message)   # what / expected / fix

Sub-packages
------------
  genai.pipeline      — CompliancePipeline + QueryResult  (start here)
  genai.core          — LLM provider, schemas, exceptions
  genai.nl2sql_agent  — six-node LangGraph NL2SQL graph (internal)
"""

from genai.pipeline import CompliancePipeline, QueryResult

__all__ = [
    "CompliancePipeline",
    "QueryResult",
]



####################################################################################################
FILE: dlh_ai\genai\pipeline.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai/pipeline.py — single entry-point for the Compliance NL2SQL workflow.

This is the *only* object a caller needs to know about.  Internal graph
nodes, agent states, LangGraph wiring, and LLM plumbing are all hidden.

Workflow (mirrors the user's mental model):
    Question → Validation → Intent → SQL → Execution → Answer

Quickstart
----------
    from dlh_ai.genai.pipeline import CompliancePipeline

    pipeline = CompliancePipeline(
        model_name = "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
        base_url   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
        api_key    = "EMPTY",
    )

    result = pipeline.ask("How many senior citizen accounts are in Mumbai circle?")

    if result.ok:
        print(result.summary)          # "Query returned 142 record(s)."
        print(result.sql)              # "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM …"
        print(result.table)            # pandas DataFrame (or None for aggregates)
    else:
        print(result.error_message)    # human-readable: what went wrong + how to fix it

Design principles (Chollet API design)
---------------------------------------
  1. Workflow-oriented — one class, one method, domain vocabulary.
  2. Low cognitive load — three constructor params, one method, one result object.
  3. Useful feedback — `QueryResult.error_message` surfaces what/expected/fix.
"""

import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import pandas as pd

from genai.core.exceptions import ModelLoadError
from genai.core.model import ModelLoaderService, create_model_service
from genai.core.processor import ComplianceProcessor
from genai.core.schemas import ComplianceChatRequest, ComplianceOrchestrationResponse
from genai.nl2sql_agent.graph import build_nl2sql_graph
from utils.config import ModelSettings
from utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.pipeline")


# ── Result object ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueryResult:
    """
    The answer to a single compliance question.

    Attributes
    ----------
    ok : bool
        True when the pipeline produced a result without errors.
    summary : str
        One-sentence human-readable answer, e.g.
        ``"Query returned 42 record(s). Columns: CUST_NBR, AGE_YRS, …"``
    sql : str | None
        The Hive SQL that was executed against the Iceberg table.
        None when intent was blocked or an error occurred before SQL generation.
    table : pd.DataFrame | None
        Query results as a DataFrame.  None for pure aggregates or on error.
    columns : list[str]
        Ordered column names matching ``table``.
    needs_clarification : bool
        True when the question was ambiguous and the bot needs more detail.
    clarification_hint : str | None
        Suggested rephrasing or guidance shown to the user.
    error_message : str | None
        Non-None on failure — structured as:
        ``"What happened. Expected: … Fix: …"``
    question_id : str
        Unique ID for this request (useful for log correlation).
    stages : list[str]
        Audit trail of internal pipeline stages (for debugging/UI pills).
    """

    ok:                  bool
    summary:             str
    sql:                 str | None                    = field(default=None)
    table:               pd.DataFrame | None           = field(default=None)
    columns:             list[str]                     = field(default_factory=list)
    needs_clarification: bool                          = field(default=False)
    clarification_hint:  str | None                   = field(default=None)
    error_message:       str | None                   = field(default=None)
    question_id:         str                           = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    stages:              list[str]                     = field(default_factory=list)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:  # pragma: no cover
        status = "ok" if self.ok else ("clarify" if self.needs_clarification else "error")
        rows   = len(self.table) if self.table is not None else 0
        return f"<QueryResult status={status} rows={rows} sql={self.sql!r:.60}>"


# ── Pipeline ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class CompliancePipeline:
    """
    End-to-end compliance NL2SQL pipeline.

    Wrap the full agent workflow in a single object that follows the user's
    mental model:

        Question  →  Validation  →  Intent  →  SQL  →  Execution  →  Answer

    Parameters
    ----------
    model_name : str
        LLM model identifier, e.g. ``"google/gemma-4-31b-it"``.
    base_url : str
        DLH inference server base URL, e.g. ``"http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"``.
    api_key : str
        Bearer token for the DLH endpoint.  Use ``"EMPTY"`` for open endpoints.
    iceberg_table : str
        Fully-qualified Iceberg table.  Defaults to ``dev_feature_store.COMP_TBL``.
    connection_name : str
        CML Data connection name used to reach Hive / Iceberg.
    verify_ssl : bool
        Set to ``True`` to enable TLS certificate verification.  Default ``False``.

    Examples
    --------
    >>> pipeline = CompliancePipeline(
    ...     model_name = "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
    ...     base_url   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
    ...     api_key    = "EMPTY",
    ... )
    >>> result = pipeline.ask("List accounts where KYC flag is N")
    >>> if result.ok:
    ...     print(result.table.head())
    ... else:
    ...     print(result.error_message)
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model_name:      str,
        base_url:        str,
        api_key:         str,
        iceberg_table:   str  = "dev_feature_store.COMP_TBL",
        connection_name: str  = "DLH-DEV-UAT - Hive on Tez",
        verify_ssl:      bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        settings = ModelSettings(
            model_name      = model_name,
            dlh_base_url    = base_url,
            dlh_api_key     = api_key,
            dlh_verify_ssl  = verify_ssl,
        )

        try:
            model_service: ModelLoaderService = create_model_service(settings)
        except ModelLoadError as exc:
            raise ModelLoadError(
                message=(
                    f"Could not connect to the LLM at {base_url!r}. "
                    f"Check the URL and API key.\n"
                    f"Expected: a reachable OpenAI-compatible endpoint.\n"
                    f"Fix: verify DLH_BASE_URL and DLH_API_KEY in your .env file."
                ),
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        graph = build_nl2sql_graph(
            model_service   = model_service,
            iceberg_table   = iceberg_table,
            connection_name = connection_name,
        )
        self._processor = ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=graph)

    def ask(self, question: str, question_id: str | None = None) -> QueryResult:
        """
        Ask a natural-language compliance question.

        Parameters
        ----------
        question : str
            Plain-English question about the compliance data, e.g.
            ``"How many senior citizen accounts are in the Delhi circle?"``
        question_id : str | None
            Optional caller-supplied key for log correlation.
            Auto-generated UUID if not provided.

        Returns
        -------
        QueryResult
            Always returns a result — never raises.  Check ``result.ok``
            before accessing ``result.table``.
        """
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(
            question    = question,
            question_id = question_id or str(uuid.uuid4()),
        )
        orchestration: ComplianceOrchestrationResponse = (
            self._processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)
        )
        return _to_query_result(orchestration)


# ── Internal converter ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _to_query_result(resp: ComplianceOrchestrationResponse) -> QueryResult:
    """Map a ComplianceOrchestrationResponse onto the user-facing QueryResult."""
    has_data  = bool(resp.df_output)
    has_error = bool(resp.error)
    ok        = has_data and not has_error and not resp.needs_clarification

    table: pd.DataFrame | None = None
    if has_data:
        try:
            table = pd.DataFrame(resp.df_output)
        except Exception:
            table = None

    summary = resp.result_summary or (
        resp.clarification_question
        or resp.error
        or "No result returned."
    )

    return QueryResult(
        ok                  = ok,
        summary             = summary,
        sql                 = resp.sql_query,
        table               = table,
        columns             = resp.result_columns or [],
        needs_clarification = resp.needs_clarification,
        clarification_hint  = resp.clarification_question,
        error_message       = resp.error,
        question_id         = resp.question_id or str(uuid.uuid4()),
        stages              = resp.stages or [],
    )

"""
genai.core — compliance orchestration, LLM provider, schemas, and exceptions.

Public surface

from ``genai.pipeline`` — it wraps everything below behind a single
workflow-oriented class.

Modules:
  exceptions.py — domain exception hierarchy (what / expected / fix)
  schemas.py    — Pydantic API models + response factory functions
  model.py      — LLM lifecycle, inference helpers, retry wrapper
  processor.py  — ComplianceProcessor orchestration (used by CompliancePipeline)
"""
